`README.md`:

```md
# reliquary-archiver

_tool to create a relic export from network packets of a certain turn-based anime game_

json output format is based on the format of [HSR-Scanner](https://github.com/kel-z/HSR-Scanner)

made to be used with [fribbels hsr optimizer](https://github.com/fribbels/hsr-optimizer)

## run

- If on Linux, or you know you want to use pcap (if you don't know what this means, you don't need to worry about it), follow the [pcap instructions](#pcap-instructions) at the bottom
- Download latest release from [here](https://github.com/IceDynamix/reliquary-archiver/releases/)
- **Launch the game and get to this screen. Do not go into the game yet**
  ![main menu start screen](./hsr_hyperdrive.jpg)
- run the archiver executable and wait until it says "Waiting for login..."
  ![archiver waiting for login](./waiting_for_login.png)
  the download button will say "Export not ready" until you start the game
- start the game by pressing "Click to Start"
- if successful, the archiver should switch to "Connected!" and the download button should become available
  ![archiver visual guide](./archiver_visual_guide.avif)
- Options:
  1. Live Import **(recommended)** - check that "Enable Live Import" is enabled in the optimizer "Import" section and that it says "Connected".
  2. Manual Import - click the download button and save the generated `archive_output-...json` file wherever you want

Troubleshooting:
- Please make sure you are fully entering the game; if you are still on the "Click to Start" train hyperdrive screen, you need to click to start.
- Brave Shield is incompatible with Live Import, please make an exception for Fribbels
- You might have to disable your VPN or enable/disable wifi!
- If you were already in-game before launching the archiver, you must log out and log back in.

alternatively, if you already have a packet capture file:

- click "Upload .pcap"
- select a `.pcap`, `.pcapng`, or `.etl` file
- click the download button once the export is ready

### cli usage

```
Usage: reliquary-archiver.exe [OPTIONS] [OUTPUT]

Arguments:
  [OUTPUT]  Path to output .json file to, per default: archive_output-%Y-%m-%dT%H-%M-%S.json

Options:
      --timeout <TIMEOUT>        How long to wait in seconds until timeout is triggered for live captures [default: 120]
  -s, --stream                   Host a websocket server to stream relic/lc updates in real-time. This will disable the timeout
  -p, --websocket-port <PORT>    Port to listen on for the websocket server, [default: 23313]
  -v, --verbose...               How verbose the output should be, can be set up to 3 times. Has no effect if RUST_LOG is set
  -l, --log-path <LOG_PATH>      Path to output log to
      --no-update                Don't check for updates, only applicable on Windows
      --always-update            Update without asking for confirmation, only applicable on Windows
      --auth-token <AUTH_TOKEN>  Github Auth token to use when checking for updates, only applicable on Windows
  -e, --exit-after-capture       Don't wait for enter to be pressed after capturing
  -H, --headless                 Run in headless mode (no GUI), only applicable when GUI feature is enabled
  -d, --detach                   Detach from the parent terminal (run in background), only applicable on Windows
  -h, --help                     Print help

Pcap Only:
      --pcap <PCAP>              Read packets from .pcap file instead of capturing live packets

Pktmon Only:
      --etl <ETL>                Read packets from .etl file instead of capturing live packets
```

to customize logging, either

- set the verbose flags
- or set `RUST_LOG` env variable to customize logging,
  see [here](https://docs.rs/tracing-subscriber/latest/tracing_subscriber/filter/struct.EnvFilter.html#directives)

to output logs to a file, provide `--log-path <path>`. file logs will always be trace-level.

## build from source

- If building on Linux, or Windows with pcap, follow instructions [here](https://github.com/rust-pcap/pcap?tab=readme-ov-file#building)
    - for me on windows, adding the `Packet.lib` and `wpcap.lib` from the sdk (check the x64 or arm dir)
      to this directory was enough to link successfully
- If building on Linux, there is also a dependency on `libwayland-dev`
- `cargo build` / `cargo run`

note that the necessary resource files are downloaded in the build script (`build.rs`) and compiled into the binary.

## pcap instructions

When running with the `pcap` feature (optional on Windows, required on Linux),
the following requirements apply:

- requires [npcap](https://npcap.com/) (windows) or `libpcap` (linux)
    - when installing on windows, make sure to enable the "winpcap api-compatible mode".
      if this is grayed out for you, see [here](https://github.com/IceDynamix/reliquary-archiver/issues/2)
      for more details
        - if you use wifi, enable `Support raw 802.11 traffic (and monitor mode) for wireless adapters`
    - when building on Linux, set the `CAP_NET_RAW` capability on the resulting executable (
      via [pcap(3pcap)](https://man.archlinux.org/man/pcap.3pcap#Under~5))
      ```sh
      sudo setcap CAP_NET_RAW=+ep target/release/reliquary-archiver
      ```

## related projects

want to do more with packet parsing? check out the
[standalone library](https://github.com/IceDynamix/reliquary) the archiver is built on top off!

looking to export your achievements? check out [stardb-exporter](https://github.com/juliuskreutz/stardb-exporter)!

```

`build.rs`:

```rs
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};
use std::env;
use std::fs::File;
use std::path::Path;

use reliquary::resource::excel::{
    AvatarConfigMap, AvatarSkillTreeConfigMap, EquipmentConfigMap, ItemConfigMap, MultiplePathAvatarConfigMap, RelicConfigMap,
    RelicMainAffixConfigMap, RelicSetConfigMap, RelicSubAffixConfigMap,
};
use reliquary::resource::{ResourceMap, TextMapEntry};
use ureq::serde::Serialize;
use ureq::serde::de::DeserializeOwned;

const GITLAB_FILES_API_PATH: &str = "projects/Dimbreath%2Fturnbasedgamedata/repository/files";
const GITHUB_RAW_URL: &str = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/DimbreathBot/TurnBasedGameData/main";

macro_rules! download_config {
    ($t:ty, $ex:expr, [$($url:expr),+ $(,)?]) => {
        write_to_out(
            {
                let mut merged = <$t>::new_empty();
                $(
                    let value = download_as_json::<$t>($url);
                    $ex(&value);
                    merged.0.extend(value.0);
                )+
                merged
            },
            <$t>::get_json_name(),
        )
    };

    ($t:ty, [$($url:expr),+ $(,)?]) => {
        download_config!($t, |_| {}, [$($url),+]);
    };

    ($t:ty, $ex:expr) => {
        download_config!($t, $ex, [&resource_path::<$t>()]);
    };

    ($t:ty) => {
        download_config!($t, |_| {}, [&resource_path::<$t>()]);
    };
}

macro_rules! download_config_and_store_text_hashes {
    ($t:ty, $field:ident, $hashes:ident, $urls:tt) => {
        download_config!(
            $t,
            |value: &$t| {
                for cfg in value.0.iter() {
                    $hashes.insert(cfg.$field);
                }
            },
            $urls
        );
    };

    ($t:ty, $field:ident, $hashes:ident) => {
        download_config_and_store_text_hashes!($t, $field, $hashes, [&resource_path::<$t>()]);
    };
}

macro_rules! download_config_and_store_partial_text_hashes {
    ($t:ty, $field:ident, $hashes:ident) => {
        download_config!($t, |value: &$t| {
            for cfg in value.0.iter() {
                if let Some(hash) = cfg.$field {
                    $hashes.insert(hash);
                }
            }
        });
    };
}

fn main() {
    println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed=Cargo.toml");

    // the text map is really, REALLY large (>25MB), so we're optimizing by only
    // keeping the entries used from relevant config files where the strings are required
    // for the export
    let mut text_hashes: HashSet<TextMapEntry> = HashSet::new();

    download_config_and_store_text_hashes!(AvatarConfigMap, AvatarName, text_hashes, [
        &resource_path::<AvatarConfigMap>(),
        &resource_path_of("AvatarConfigLD.json"),
    ]);
    download_config_and_store_text_hashes!(EquipmentConfigMap, EquipmentName, text_hashes);
    download_config_and_store_text_hashes!(RelicSetConfigMap, SetName, text_hashes);
    download_config_and_store_partial_text_hashes!(ItemConfigMap, ItemName, text_hashes);

    download_config!(AvatarSkillTreeConfigMap, [
        &resource_path::<AvatarSkillTreeConfigMap>(),
        &resource_path_of("AvatarSkillTreeConfigLD.json"),
    ]);
    download_config!(MultiplePathAvatarConfigMap);
    download_config!(RelicConfigMap);
    download_config!(RelicMainAffixConfigMap);
    download_config!(RelicSubAffixConfigMap);

    save_text_map(&text_hashes, "EN");

    #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
    {
        let mut res = winres::WindowsResource::new();
        res.set_icon("assets/icon.ico").set("InternalName", "Reliquary Archiver");
        res.compile().unwrap();
    }
}

fn save_text_map(hashes: &HashSet<TextMapEntry>, language: &str) {
    let hashes: HashSet<String> = hashes.iter().map(|k| k.Hash.to_string()).collect();

    let file_name = format!("TextMap{language}.json");

    let text_map: HashMap<String, String> = download_as_json::<HashMap<String, String>>(&format!("TextMap/{file_name}"))
        .into_iter()
        .filter(|(k, _)| hashes.contains(k))
        .collect();

    write_to_out(text_map, &file_name);
}

fn resource_path<T: ResourceMap>() -> String {
    resource_path_of(T::get_json_name())
}

fn resource_path_of(name: &str) -> String {
    format!("ExcelOutput/{name}")
}

/// Downloads a file from the game data repo, given its path within the repo.
///
/// Tries GitLab through glab (authenticated, avoids rate limits), then GitLab
/// unauthenticated, then the GitHub mirror.
fn download_as_json<T: DeserializeOwned>(path: &str) -> T {
    let gitlab_api_path = format!("{GITLAB_FILES_API_PATH}/{}/raw?ref=main", path.replace('/', "%2F"));

    if let Some(value) = download_with_glab(&gitlab_api_path) {
        return value;
    }

    let urls = [
        format!("https://gitlab.com/api/v4/{gitlab_api_path}"),
        format!("{GITHUB_RAW_URL}/{path}"),
    ];
    let mut errors = Vec::new();
    for url in &urls {
        match download_with_ureq(url) {
            Ok(value) => return value,
            Err(e) => {
                println!("cargo:warning=failed to download {url}: {e}");
                errors.push(format!("{url}: {e}"));
            }
        }
    }

    panic!("Failed to download {path} from any source:\n{}", errors.join("\n"));
}

fn download_with_ureq<T: DeserializeOwned>(url: &str) -> Result<T, String> {
    ureq::get(url)
        .call()
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
        .into_json()
        .map_err(|e| format!("invalid json: {e}"))
}

fn download_with_glab<T: DeserializeOwned>(api_path: &str) -> Option<T> {
    if !glab_available() {
        return None;
    }

    // --hostname, since glab would otherwise use this repo's (GitHub) remote
    let output = match std::process::Command::new("glab")
        .args(["api", "--hostname", "gitlab.com", api_path])
        .output()
    {
        Ok(output) => output,
        Err(e) => {
            println!("cargo:warning=glab failed to run ({e}), downloading {api_path} unauthenticated");
            return None;
        }
    };

    if !output.status.success() {
        let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
        println!(
            "cargo:warning=glab api failed ({}), downloading {api_path} unauthenticated",
            stderr.trim()
        );
        return None;
    }

    match ureq::serde_json::from_slice(&output.stdout) {
        Ok(value) => Some(value),
        Err(e) => {
            println!("cargo:warning=glab returned invalid json ({e}), downloading {api_path} unauthenticated");
            None
        }
    }
}

fn glab_available() -> bool {
    static GLAB: std::sync::OnceLock<bool> = std::sync::OnceLock::new();

    *GLAB.get_or_init(|| {
        std::process::Command::new("glab")
            .arg("--version")
            .output()
            .is_ok_and(|o| o.status.success())
    })
}

fn write_to_out<T: DeserializeOwned + Serialize>(value: T, file_name: &str) {
    let out_dir = env::var_os("OUT_DIR").unwrap();
    let out_path = Path::new(&out_dir).join(file_name);

    let mut file = File::create(out_path).unwrap();

    ureq::serde_json::to_writer(&mut file, &value).unwrap();
}

`src/accounts.rs`:

```rs
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;

use futures::lock::Mutex;
use reliquary_archiver::export::fribbels::{OptimizerEvent, OptimizerExporter};
use tokio::sync::broadcast;
#[cfg(feature = "stream")]
use tokio::task::JoinHandle;
use tracing::info;
#[cfg(feature = "stream")]
use tracing::warn;

// Not every build has a subscriber that looks at the uid of lifecycle events
#[allow(dead_code)]
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum AccountEvent {
    Discovered { uid: u32 },
    Reconnected { uid: u32 },
    ExporterEvent { uid: u32, event: OptimizerEvent },
}

/// Manages multiple account exporters, one per conversation.
/// Maps conv_id -> exporter and uid -> conv_id for account tracking.
pub struct MultiAccountManager {
    /// All exporters indexed by conversation ID
    exporters: HashMap<u32, Arc<Mutex<OptimizerExporter>>>,

    /// Track which conversation belongs to which account UID
    uid_to_conv: HashMap<u32, u32>,

    /// Unified event channel for all account lifecycle and exporter events
    event_tx: broadcast::Sender<AccountEvent>,

    /// Task handles for exporter event forwarding (internal management)
    #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
    subscription_tasks: HashMap<u32, JoinHandle<()>>,
}

impl MultiAccountManager {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        let (event_tx, _) = broadcast::channel(100);
        Self {
            exporters: HashMap::new(),
            uid_to_conv: HashMap::new(),
            event_tx,
            #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
            subscription_tasks: HashMap::new(),
        }
    }

    /// Subscribe to all account events (discoveries, reconnections, exporter events)
    #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
    pub fn subscribe(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<AccountEvent> {
        self.event_tx.subscribe()
    }

    /// Get or create an exporter for a conversation ID
    pub fn get_or_create_exporter(&mut self, conv_id: u32) -> Arc<Mutex<OptimizerExporter>> {
        self.exporters
            .entry(conv_id)
            .or_insert_with(|| Arc::new(Mutex::new(OptimizerExporter::new())))
            .clone()
    }

    /// Register a UID for a conversation. If the UID was previously associated
    /// with a different conversation, delete the old exporter and emit reconnection event.
    pub fn register_uid(&mut self, conv_id: u32, uid: u32) {
        let event = match self.uid_to_conv.insert(uid, conv_id) {
            // Same conv_id, same UID - no-op
            Some(old_conv_id) if old_conv_id == conv_id => return,
            Some(old_conv_id) => {
                // Same UID reconnecting on a different conversation - delete old exporter
                info!(
                    uid,
                    old_conv = old_conv_id,
                    new_conv = conv_id,
                    "account reconnected, replacing old exporter"
                );
                self.exporters.remove(&old_conv_id);
                AccountEvent::Reconnected { uid }
            }
            None => {
                info!(uid, conv_id, "new account discovered");
                AccountEvent::Discovered { uid }
            }
        };

        #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
        if let Some(exporter) = self.get_account_exporter(uid) {
            self.spawn_exporter_subscription(uid, exporter);
        }

        self.event_tx.send(event).ok();
    }

    /// Get the exporter for a specific UID
    pub fn get_account_exporter(&self, uid: u32) -> Option<Arc<Mutex<OptimizerExporter>>> {
        self.uid_to_conv.get(&uid).and_then(|conv_id| self.exporters.get(conv_id)).cloned()
    }

    /// Get the exporter of any one account, for when the caller doesn't care which
    #[cfg(feature = "gui")]
    pub fn get_first_account_exporter(&self) -> Option<Arc<Mutex<OptimizerExporter>>> {
        self.uid_to_conv.values().find_map(|conv_id| self.exporters.get(conv_id)).cloned()
    }

    /// Spawn a task to forward events from an exporter's channel to the manager's unified event channel
    #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
    fn spawn_exporter_subscription(&mut self, uid: u32, exporter: Arc<Mutex<OptimizerExporter>>) {
        use reliquary_archiver::export::Exporter;
        use tokio::sync::broadcast::error::RecvError;

        let event_tx = self.event_tx.clone();

        let task = tokio::spawn(async move {
            let (initial_event, mut rx) = exporter.lock().await.subscribe();

            // Send initial event if available
            if let Some(event) = initial_event {
                event_tx.send(AccountEvent::ExporterEvent { uid, event }).ok();
            }

            // Forward all subsequent events
            loop {
                match rx.recv().await {
                    Ok(event) => {
                        if event_tx.send(AccountEvent::ExporterEvent { uid, event }).is_err() {
                            warn!(uid, "manager event channel closed");
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    // Missing a few updates is recoverable for subscribers, losing the subscription is not
                    Err(RecvError::Lagged(skipped)) => {
                        warn!(uid, skipped, "exporter subscription lagged, some events were dropped");
                    }
                    Err(RecvError::Closed) => {
                        warn!(uid, "exporter subscription closed");
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        });

        // Store task handle (abort old one if exists)
        if let Some(old_task) = self.subscription_tasks.insert(uid, task) {
            old_task.abort();
        }
    }
}

```

`src/capture/mod.rs`:

```rs
//! Packet sources.
//!
//! Everything that can produce game traffic implements [`PacketSource`], which opens into a
//! [`PacketStream`] of Ethernet frames. Callers describe what they want with a [`SourceSpec`]
//! and call [`open_source`]; which backend ends up serving it is decided here and nowhere else.
//!
//! Live backends only need to implement [`DeviceBackend`], [`DeviceSource`] takes care of
//! device discovery and merging. File based sources implement [`PacketSource`] directly.

use std::error::Error;
use std::fmt::{Debug, Display};
use std::path::PathBuf;

use futures::StreamExt;
use futures::stream::BoxStream;
use tokio::sync::mpsc;
use tokio_stream::StreamMap;
use tokio_stream::wrappers::{ReceiverStream, UnboundedReceiverStream};
use tracing::instrument;

#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "pktmon"))]
pub mod pktmon;

#[cfg(feature = "pcap")]
pub mod pcap;

/// The UDP ports the game talks to its servers on
const GAME_PORTS: (u16, u16) = (23301, 23302);

type BoxError = Box<dyn Error + Send + Sync>;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum CaptureError {
    /// The device or file could not be opened
    Device(BoxError),
    /// The packet filter could not be applied
    Filter(BoxError),
    /// Reading packets failed
    Capture(BoxError),
}

impl Display for CaptureError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            CaptureError::Device(e) => write!(f, "could not open capture source: {e}"),
            CaptureError::Filter(e) => write!(f, "could not apply capture filter: {e}"),
            CaptureError::Capture(e) => write!(f, "capture failed: {e}"),
        }
    }
}

impl Error for CaptureError {
    fn source(&self) -> Option<&(dyn Error + 'static)> {
        match self {
            CaptureError::Device(e) | CaptureError::Filter(e) | CaptureError::Capture(e) => Some(e.as_ref()),
        }
    }
}

pub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, CaptureError>;

/// Represents a captured network packet (an Ethernet frame)
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Packet {
    pub source_id: u64,
    pub data: Vec<u8>,
}

/// A stream of captured packets. Ends when the source is exhausted, which never happens for live captures.
pub type PacketStream = BoxStream<'static, Result<Packet>>;

/// Anything that can produce game traffic
pub trait PacketSource: Send + 'static {
    /// Start reading packets from this source
    fn open(self: Box<Self>) -> Result<PacketStream>;
}

/// Describes where packets should be read from
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum SourceSpec {
    /// Capture live traffic from the network
    Live,
    /// Read a recorded .pcap file
    #[cfg(feature = "pcap")]
    PcapFile(PathBuf),
    /// Read a recorded .etl file
    #[cfg(feature = "pktmon")]
    EtlFile(PathBuf),
}

impl SourceSpec {
    pub fn is_live(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, SourceSpec::Live)
    }

    /// The extensions of the capture files that can be read
    #[cfg(feature = "gui")]
    pub fn file_extensions() -> Vec<&'static str> {
        let mut extensions = Vec::new();
        #[cfg(feature = "pcap")]
        extensions.extend(["pcap", "pcapng"]);
        #[cfg(feature = "pktmon")]
        extensions.push("etl");
        extensions
    }

    /// Picks the source that can read the capture file, going by its extension
    #[cfg(feature = "gui")]
    pub fn for_file(path: PathBuf) -> Option<Self> {
        let extension = path.extension()?.to_str()?.to_ascii_lowercase();
        match extension.as_str() {
            #[cfg(feature = "pcap")]
            "pcap" | "pcapng" => Some(SourceSpec::PcapFile(path)),
            #[cfg(feature = "pktmon")]
            "etl" => Some(SourceSpec::EtlFile(path)),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
}

/// Open the packet source described by `spec`
pub fn open_source(spec: &SourceSpec) -> Result<PacketStream> {
    let source: Box<dyn PacketSource> = match spec {
        SourceSpec::Live => live_source(),
        #[cfg(feature = "pcap")]
        SourceSpec::PcapFile(path) => Box::new(pcap::PcapFileSource::new(path.clone())),
        #[cfg(feature = "pktmon")]
        SourceSpec::EtlFile(path) => Box::new(pktmon::EtlFileSource::new(path.clone())),
    };

    source.open()
}

fn live_source() -> Box<dyn PacketSource> {
    #[cfg(feature = "pcap")]
    {
        Box::new(DeviceSource(pcap::PcapBackend))
    }

    #[cfg(all(not(feature = "pcap"), feature = "pktmon"))]
    {
        Box::new(DeviceSource(pktmon::PktmonBackend))
    }
}

/// A live capture backend that reads from one or more network devices
pub trait DeviceBackend: Send + 'static {
    type Device: Send;

    /// List all available capture devices
    fn list_devices(&self) -> Result<Vec<Self::Device>>;

    /// Get the name of the device
    fn device_name(device: &Self::Device) -> String;

    /// Start capturing packets on the device
    fn open_device(device: &Self::Device) -> Result<PacketStream>;
}

/// Captures from all devices of a [`DeviceBackend`], picking up devices that appear later
pub struct DeviceSource<B>(pub B);

impl<B: DeviceBackend> PacketSource for DeviceSource<B> {
    #[instrument(skip_all)]
    fn open(self: Box<Self>) -> Result<PacketStream> {
        use tokio::time::{Duration, interval};

        let backend = self.0;

        if backend.list_devices()?.is_empty() {
            tracing::warn!("Could not find any network devices");
        }

        // Create a channel to forward packets
        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();

        // Spawn task to manage stream and discover new devices
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let mut merged_stream = StreamMap::new();
            let mut check_interval = interval(Duration::from_secs(10));

            loop {
                tokio::select! {
                    // Forward packets from merged stream
                    Some((_, item)) = merged_stream.next() => {
                        if let Err(e) = &item {
                            tracing::warn!(%e);
                        }

                        if tx.send(item).is_err() {
                            break; // Receiver dropped
                        }
                    }

                    // Check for new devices every 10 seconds
                    _ = check_interval.tick() => add_new_devices(&backend, &mut merged_stream),
                }
            }
        });

        Ok(UnboundedReceiverStream::new(rx).boxed())
    }
}

fn add_new_devices<B: DeviceBackend>(backend: &B, streams: &mut StreamMap<String, PacketStream>) {
    let devices = match backend.list_devices() {
        Ok(devices) => devices,
        Err(e) => {
            tracing::warn!("Failed to list devices during periodic check: {:#?}", e);
            return;
        }
    };

    for device in devices {
        let device_name = B::device_name(&device);
        if streams.contains_key(&device_name) {
            continue;
        }

        tracing::info!("Discovered new device: {}", device_name);

        match B::open_device(&device) {
            Ok(stream) => {
                streams.insert(device_name, stream);
            }
            Err(e) => {
                tracing::warn!("Capture initialization error on new device {}: {:#?}", device_name, e);
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Runs a blocking packet reader on a worker thread and exposes what it reads as a stream.
///
/// `read` is handed a callback to pass packets to, which returns `false` once nobody is listening anymore.
fn blocking_stream(read: impl FnOnce(&mut dyn FnMut(Packet) -> bool) + Send + 'static) -> PacketStream {
    let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel(1024);

    tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || read(&mut |packet| tx.blocking_send(Ok(packet)).is_ok()));

    ReceiverStream::new(rx).boxed()
}

/// Derives a stable source id from whatever identifies the source, like a device name or a file path
fn source_id(key: impl std::hash::Hash) -> u64 {
    use std::hash::{DefaultHasher, Hasher};

    let mut hasher = DefaultHasher::new();
    key.hash(&mut hasher);
    hasher.finish()
}

`src/capture/pcap.rs`:

```rs
use std::future;
use std::path::PathBuf;

use ::pcap::{self, Capture, Device as PcapDevice};
use futures::StreamExt;
use pcap::PacketCodec;
use tracing::{info, instrument, warn};

use super::*;

pub struct PcapBackend;

impl DeviceBackend for PcapBackend {
    type Device = PcapDevice;

    fn list_devices(&self) -> Result<Vec<Self::Device>> {
        Ok(PcapDevice::list()
            .map_err(|e| CaptureError::Device(Box::new(e)))?
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|d| matches!(d.flags.connection_status, pcap::ConnectionStatus::Connected))
            .filter(|d| !d.addresses.is_empty())
            .filter(|d| !d.flags.is_loopback())
            .collect::<Vec<_>>())
    }

    fn device_name(device: &Self::Device) -> String {
        device.name.clone()
    }

    #[instrument(skip_all, fields(device = device.desc))]
    fn open_device(device: &Self::Device) -> Result<PacketStream> {
        let capture = Capture::from_device(device.clone())
            .map_err(|e| CaptureError::Device(Box::new(e)))?
            .immediate_mode(true)
            .promisc(true)
            .timeout(1000)
            .buffer_size(1024 * 1024 * 16) // 16MB
            .open()
            .map_err(|e| CaptureError::Capture(Box::new(e)))?;

        let mut capture = capture.setnonblock().map_err(|e| CaptureError::Capture(Box::new(e)))?;

        capture
            .filter(&pcap_filter(), true)
            .map_err(|e| CaptureError::Filter(Box::new(e)))?;

        let source_id = source_id(&device.name);

        let device_name = device.name.clone();
        let stream = capture
            .stream(Codec { source_id })
            .map_err(|e| CaptureError::Capture(Box::new(e)))?;

        Ok(stream
            .take_while(move |r| {
                future::ready(match r {
                    // Check if this is a device removal error
                    Err(pcap::Error::PcapError(error_msg)) if error_msg.contains("ERROR_DEVICE_REMOVED") => {
                        warn!(%error_msg, %device_name, "Device removed, terminating capture stream");
                        false // Stop the stream immediately
                    }
                    _ => true, // Continue for Ok packets and other errors
                })
            })
            .map(|r| r.map_err(|e| CaptureError::Capture(Box::new(e))))
            .boxed())
    }
}

pub struct Codec {
    source_id: u64,
}

impl PacketCodec for Codec {
    type Item = Packet;

    fn decode(&mut self, pkt: pcap::Packet) -> Self::Item {
        Packet {
            source_id: self.source_id,
            data: pkt.data.to_vec(),
        }
    }
}

/// Reads packets from a recorded .pcap file
pub struct PcapFileSource {
    path: PathBuf,
}

impl PcapFileSource {
    pub fn new(path: PathBuf) -> Self {
        Self { path }
    }
}

impl PacketSource for PcapFileSource {
    #[instrument(skip_all)]
    fn open(self: Box<Self>) -> Result<PacketStream> {
        info!("Capturing from pcap file: {}", self.path.display());
        let mut capture = Capture::from_file(&self.path).map_err(|e| CaptureError::Device(Box::new(e)))?;
        let linktype = capture.get_datalink();
        capture
            .filter(&pcap_filter(), false)
            .map_err(|e| CaptureError::Filter(Box::new(e)))?;

        let source_id = source_id(&self.path);

        Ok(blocking_stream(move |send| {
            while let Ok(packet) = capture.next_packet() {
                let Ok(data) = normalize_offline_pcap_payload(linktype, packet.data) else {
                    continue;
                };

                if !send(Packet { source_id, data }) {
                    break;
                }
            }
        }))
    }
}

/// Only lets the game's traffic through
fn pcap_filter() -> String {
    format!("udp portrange {}-{}", GAME_PORTS.0, GAME_PORTS.1)
}

/// Turns a packet read from a file into an Ethernet frame, which is what the sniffer expects
fn normalize_offline_pcap_payload(linktype: pcap::Linktype, payload: &[u8]) -> std::result::Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
    fn frame_ip_payload(payload: &[u8], ethertype: u16) -> Vec<u8> {
        // Use a synthetic Ethernet header so the downstream sniffer can parse RAW/IP captures.
        let mut framed = Vec::with_capacity(14 + payload.len());
        framed.extend_from_slice(&[0u8; 12]);
        framed.extend_from_slice(&ethertype.to_be_bytes());
        framed.extend_from_slice(payload);
        framed
    }

    let link_name = linktype.get_name().ok();
    let is_ethernet = linktype == pcap::Linktype::ETHERNET || link_name.as_deref() == Some("EN10MB");
    let is_ipv4 = linktype == pcap::Linktype::IPV4 || link_name.as_deref() == Some("IPV4");
    let is_ipv6 = linktype == pcap::Linktype::IPV6 || link_name.as_deref() == Some("IPV6");
    let is_raw_ip = linktype == pcap::Linktype::RAW || linktype.0 == 12 || link_name.as_deref() == Some("RAW");

    if is_ethernet {
        return Ok(payload.to_vec());
    }

    if payload.is_empty() {
        return Err("packet payload is empty".to_string());
    }

    if is_ipv4 {
        return Ok(frame_ip_payload(payload, 0x0800));
    }

    if is_ipv6 {
        return Ok(frame_ip_payload(payload, 0x86DD));
    }

    if is_raw_ip {
        let version = payload[0] >> 4;
        return match version {
            4 => Ok(frame_ip_payload(payload, 0x0800)),
            6 => Ok(frame_ip_payload(payload, 0x86DD)),
            _ => Err(format!("RAW packet does not start with IPv4/IPv6 header (version={version})")),
        };
    }

    let link_name = link_name.unwrap_or_else(|| "UNKNOWN".to_string());
    Err(format!("unsupported pcap datalink {link_name} ({})", linktype.0))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use ::pcap::Linktype;

    use super::normalize_offline_pcap_payload;

    #[test]
    fn ethernet_frames_are_passed_through() {
        let frame = vec![1, 2, 3, 4];
        assert_eq!(normalize_offline_pcap_payload(Linktype::ETHERNET, &frame).unwrap(), frame);
    }

    #[test]
    fn raw_ip_packets_get_a_synthetic_ethernet_header() {
        let ipv4 = [0x45, 0, 0, 20];
        let framed = normalize_offline_pcap_payload(Linktype::RAW, &ipv4).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(framed.len(), 14 + ipv4.len());
        assert_eq!(&framed[12..14], &[0x08, 0x00]);
        assert_eq!(&framed[14..], &ipv4);

        let ipv6 = [0x60, 0, 0, 0];
        let framed = normalize_offline_pcap_payload(Linktype::RAW, &ipv6).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(&framed[12..14], &[0x86, 0xDD]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn unusable_packets_are_rejected() {
        assert!(normalize_offline_pcap_payload(Linktype::RAW, &[]).is_err());
        assert!(normalize_offline_pcap_payload(Linktype::RAW, &[0x10]).is_err());
        assert!(normalize_offline_pcap_payload(Linktype::NULL, &[0x45]).is_err());
    }
}

`src/capture/pktmon.rs`:

```rs
use std::future;
use std::path::PathBuf;

use ::pktmon::filter::{PktMonFilter, TransportProtocol};
use ::pktmon::{Capture, EtlCapture, PacketPayload};
use futures::StreamExt;
use tracing::{info, instrument, warn};

use super::*;

pub struct PktmonBackend;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct PktmonCaptureDevice;

impl DeviceBackend for PktmonBackend {
    type Device = PktmonCaptureDevice;

    fn list_devices(&self) -> Result<Vec<Self::Device>> {
        // PktMon doesn't need device selection - it captures all interfaces
        Ok(vec![PktmonCaptureDevice])
    }

    fn device_name(_device: &Self::Device) -> String {
        "pktmon".to_string()
    }

    #[instrument(skip_all)]
    fn open_device(_device: &Self::Device) -> Result<PacketStream> {
        let mut capture = Capture::new().map_err(|e| CaptureError::Capture(Box::new(e)))?;

        for port in [GAME_PORTS.0, GAME_PORTS.1] {
            let filter = PktMonFilter {
                name: "UDP Filter".to_string(),
                transport_protocol: Some(TransportProtocol::UDP),
                port: port.into(),
                ..PktMonFilter::default()
            };

            capture.add_filter(filter).map_err(|e| CaptureError::Filter(Box::new(e)))?;
        }

        capture.start().map_err(|e| CaptureError::Capture(Box::new(e)))?;

        let stream = capture.stream().map_err(|e| CaptureError::Capture(Box::new(e)))?;

        Ok(stream
            .filter_map(|packet| {
                future::ready(match packet.payload {
                    PacketPayload::Ethernet(payload) => Some(Ok(Packet {
                        source_id: packet.component_id as u64,
                        data: payload,
                    })),
                    _ => None,
                })
            })
            .boxed())
    }
}

/// Reads packets from a recorded .etl file
pub struct EtlFileSource {
    path: PathBuf,
}

impl EtlFileSource {
    pub fn new(path: PathBuf) -> Self {
        Self { path }
    }
}

impl PacketSource for EtlFileSource {
    #[instrument(skip_all)]
    fn open(self: Box<Self>) -> Result<PacketStream> {
        info!("Capturing from etl file: {}", self.path.display());
        let mut capture = EtlCapture::new(&self.path).map_err(|e| CaptureError::Device(Box::new(e)))?;
        capture.start().map_err(|e| CaptureError::Capture(Box::new(e)))?;

        let source_id = source_id(&self.path);

        Ok(blocking_stream(move |send| {
            while let Ok(packet) = capture.next_packet() {
                if let PacketPayload::Ethernet(data) = packet.payload {
                    if !send(Packet { source_id, data }) {
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            if let Err(e) = capture.stop() {
                warn!(%e, "could not stop etl capture");
            }
        }))
    }
}

`src/cli.rs`:

```rs
//! Headless mode: capture until the export is complete, then write it to a file.

use std::error::Error;
use std::fs::File;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use chrono::Local;
use futures::lock::Mutex;
use futures::{FutureExt, StreamExt, future, select};
use reliquary_archiver::export::fribbels::Export;
use tokio::pin;
use tracing::{error, info, instrument, warn};

use crate::Args;
use crate::accounts::MultiAccountManager;
use crate::capture::{self, SourceSpec};
use crate::session::{CaptureSession, SessionEvent};

pub async fn run(args: Args) {
    let source = source_from_args(&args);

    match capture(&args, &source).await {
        Some(export) => write_output(args.output, &export),
        None => warn!("skipped writing output"),
    }

    if let Some(log_path) = args.log_path {
        info!("wrote logs to {}", log_path.display());
    }
}

fn source_from_args(args: &Args) -> SourceSpec {
    #[cfg(feature = "pcap")]
    if let Some(path) = &args.pcap {
        return SourceSpec::PcapFile(path.clone());
    }

    #[cfg(feature = "pktmon")]
    if let Some(path) = &args.etl {
        return SourceSpec::EtlFile(path.clone());
    }

    SourceSpec::Live
}

#[instrument(skip_all)]
async fn capture(args: &Args, source: &SourceSpec) -> Option<Export> {
    let manager = Arc::new(Mutex::new(MultiAccountManager::new()));

    // Recorded captures are read from start to end, streaming only makes sense for live traffic
    #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
    let selected_account_tx = (args.stream && source.is_live()).then(|| {
        use crate::websocket::{PortSource, start_websocket_server};

        let (tx, rx) = tokio::sync::watch::channel::<Option<u32>>(None);

        tokio::spawn(start_websocket_server(PortSource::Fixed(args.websocket_port), manager.clone(), rx));

        info!("WebSocket server starting on port {}...", args.websocket_port);
        tx
    });

    #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
    let streaming = selected_account_tx.is_some();

    #[cfg(not(feature = "stream"))]
    let streaming = false;

    let mut packets = capture::open_source(source).expect("Failed to start packet capture").fuse();

    let timeout = if !source.is_live() {
        None
    } else if streaming {
        info!("WebSocket streaming enabled - capture will run until manually stopped");
        None
    } else {
        info!("listening with a timeout of {} seconds...", args.timeout);
        Some(Duration::from_secs(args.timeout))
    };

    if source.is_live() {
        info!("instructions: go to main menu screen and go to the \"Click to Start\" screen");
    }

    pin!(
        let timeout_future = maybe_timeout(timeout).fuse();
    );

    let mut session = CaptureSession::new(manager, true);

    'recv: loop {
        let packet = select! {
            packet = packets.next() => match packet {
                Some(Ok(packet)) => packet,
                Some(Err(e)) => {
                    warn!(%e);
                    break 'recv;
                }
                None => break 'recv,
            },

            _ = timeout_future => break 'recv,
        };

        for event in session.handle_packet(packet).await {
            match event {
                SessionEvent::AccountSeen { uid } => {
                    #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
                    if let Some(tx) = &selected_account_tx {
                        tx.send(Some(uid)).ok();
                        info!(uid, "Auto-selected account for WebSocket streaming");
                    }

                    #[cfg(not(feature = "stream"))]
                    let _ = uid;
                }
                SessionEvent::LoginFinished if !streaming => {
                    info!("detected login end, assume initialization is finished");
                    break 'recv;
                }
                SessionEvent::DecryptionKeyMissing => {
                    warn!(
                        "This game version is not supported yet. It usually takes a few days for Reliquary Archiver to get updated for new versions. Please try again at a later point."
                    );
                    break 'recv;
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        // Check if initialized for early exit in non-streaming mode
        if !streaming && session.is_initialized().await {
            info!("retrieved all relevant packets, stop listening");
            break 'recv;
        }
    }

    session.export().await
}

async fn maybe_timeout(timeout: Option<Duration>) {
    if let Some(timeout) = timeout {
        tokio::time::sleep(timeout).await;
    } else {
        future::pending::<()>().await;
    }
}

fn write_output(output: Option<PathBuf>, export: &Export) {
    let default_name = Local::now().format("archive_output-%Y-%m-%dT%H-%M-%S.json").to_string();
    let mut output_file = output.unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from(&default_name));

    info!("exporting collected data");
    loop {
        match write_json(&output_file, export) {
            Ok(()) => {
                let written_to = output_file.canonicalize().unwrap_or(output_file);
                info!("wrote output to {}", written_to.display());
                return;
            }
            Err(e) => {
                error!("Failed to write to {}: {}", output_file.display(), e);

                match pick_alternative_path(&default_name) {
                    Some(new_path) => output_file = new_path,
                    None => {
                        error!("No alternative path selected, aborting write");
                        return;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn write_json(path: &Path, export: &Export) -> Result<(), Box<dyn Error>> {
    let file = File::create(path)?;
    serde_json::to_writer_pretty(&file, export)?;
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
fn pick_alternative_path(file_name: &str) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    rfd::FileDialog::new()
        .set_title("Select output file location")
        .set_file_name(file_name)
        .add_filter("JSON files", &["json"])
        .save_file()
}

#[cfg(not(feature = "gui"))]
fn pick_alternative_path(_file_name: &str) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    None
}

`src/elevation.rs`:

```rs
//! Administrator checks for the pktmon backend, which can't capture without elevated privileges.

use tracing::error;

pub fn is_admin() -> bool {
    unsafe { windows::Win32::UI::Shell::IsUserAnAdmin().into() }
}

/// Explains why administrative privileges are needed and offers to restart the application with them
pub fn prompt_and_escalate() {
    println!();
    println!("===========================================================================================================");
    println!("                       Administrative privileges are required to capture packets");
    println!("===========================================================================================================");
    println!();
    println!("Reliquary Archiver now uses PacketMonitor (pktmon) to capture the game traffic instead of npcap on Windows");
    println!("Due to the way pktmon works, it requires running the application as an administrator");
    println!();
    println!("If you don't feel comfortable running the application as an administrator, you can download the pcap");
    println!("version of Reliquary Archiver from the GitHub releases page.");
    println!();
    if confirm("Would you like to restart the application with elevated privileges? (Y/n): ") {
        if let Err(e) = escalate_to_admin() {
            error!("Failed to escalate privileges: {}", e);
        }
    }
}

fn confirm(msg: &str) -> bool {
    use std::io::Write;

    print!("{}", msg);
    if std::io::stdout().flush().is_err() {
        return false;
    }

    let mut input = String::new();
    if std::io::stdin().read_line(&mut input).is_err() {
        return false;
    }

    input = input.trim().to_lowercase();
    input.starts_with("y") || input.is_empty()
}

fn escalate_to_admin() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    use std::os::windows::ffi::OsStrExt;

    use windows::Win32::System::Console::GetConsoleWindow;
    use windows::Win32::UI::Shell::{SEE_MASK_NO_CONSOLE, SEE_MASK_NOCLOSEPROCESS, SHELLEXECUTEINFOW, ShellExecuteExW};
    use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{GW_OWNER, GetWindow, SW_SHOWNORMAL};
    use windows::core::{PCWSTR, w};

    let args_str = std::env::args().skip(1).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ");

    let exe_path = std::env::current_exe()
        .expect("Failed to get current exe")
        .as_os_str()
        .encode_wide()
        .chain(Some(0))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let args = args_str.encode_utf16().chain(Some(0)).collect::<Vec<_>>();

    unsafe {
        let mut options = SHELLEXECUTEINFOW {
            cbSize: size_of::<SHELLEXECUTEINFOW>() as u32,
            fMask: SEE_MASK_NOCLOSEPROCESS | SEE_MASK_NO_CONSOLE,
            hwnd: GetWindow(GetConsoleWindow(), GW_OWNER).unwrap_or(GetConsoleWindow()),
            lpVerb: w!("runas"),
            lpFile: PCWSTR(exe_path.as_ptr()),
            lpParameters: PCWSTR(args.as_ptr()),
            lpDirectory: PCWSTR::null(),
            nShow: SW_SHOWNORMAL.0,
            lpIDList: std::ptr::null_mut(),
            lpClass: PCWSTR::null(),
            dwHotKey: 0,
            ..Default::default()
        };

        ShellExecuteExW(&mut options)?;
    };

    // Exit the current process since we launched a new elevated one
    std::process::exit(0);
}

`src/export/database.rs`:

```rs
use std::sync::OnceLock;

use reliquary::resource::excel::{
    AvatarConfigMap, AvatarSkillTreeConfigMap, EquipmentConfigMap, ItemConfigMap, MultiplePathAvatarConfigMap, RelicConfigMap,
    RelicMainAffixConfigMap, RelicSetConfigMap, RelicSubAffixConfigMap,
};
use reliquary::resource::text_map::TextMap;
use serde::de::DeserializeOwned;
use tracing::{info, instrument};

pub struct Database {
    pub avatar_config: AvatarConfigMap,
    pub avatar_skill_tree_config: AvatarSkillTreeConfigMap,
    pub equipment_config: EquipmentConfigMap,
    pub item_config: ItemConfigMap,
    pub multipath_avatar_config: MultiplePathAvatarConfigMap,
    pub relic_config: RelicConfigMap,
    pub relic_set_config: RelicSetConfigMap,
    pub relic_main_affix_config: RelicMainAffixConfigMap,
    pub relic_sub_affix_config: RelicSubAffixConfigMap,
    pub text_map: TextMap,
}

pub fn get_database() -> &'static Database {
    static DATABASE: OnceLock<Database> = OnceLock::new();
    DATABASE.get_or_init(Database::new)
}

impl Default for Database {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl Database {
    #[instrument(name = "config_map")]
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        info!("using local database");

        // config files are downloaded by the build script
        //
        // i *would* create a fn load_local_config<T: ResourceMap + DeserializeOwned>()
        // to avoid duplicating the json file names by using T::get_json_name,
        // but concat!() only takes string literals. it doesn't even take `&'static str`!!
        // https://github.com/rust-lang/rust/issues/53749
        Database {
            avatar_config: Self::parse_json(include_str!(concat!(env!("OUT_DIR"), "/AvatarConfig.json"))),
            avatar_skill_tree_config: Self::parse_json(include_str!(concat!(env!("OUT_DIR"), "/AvatarSkillTreeConfig.json"))),
            equipment_config: Self::parse_json(include_str!(concat!(env!("OUT_DIR"), "/EquipmentConfig.json"))),
            item_config: Self::parse_json(include_str!(concat!(env!("OUT_DIR"), "/ItemConfig.json"))),
            multipath_avatar_config: Self::parse_json(include_str!(concat!(env!("OUT_DIR"), "/MultiplePathAvatarConfig.json"))),
            relic_config: Self::parse_json(include_str!(concat!(env!("OUT_DIR"), "/RelicConfig.json"))),
            relic_set_config: Self::parse_json(include_str!(concat!(env!("OUT_DIR"), "/RelicSetConfig.json"))),
            relic_main_affix_config: Self::parse_json(include_str!(concat!(env!("OUT_DIR"), "/RelicMainAffixConfig.json"))),
            relic_sub_affix_config: Self::parse_json(include_str!(concat!(env!("OUT_DIR"), "/RelicSubAffixConfig.json"))),
            text_map: Self::parse_json(include_str!(concat!(env!("OUT_DIR"), "/TextMapEN.json"))),
        }
    }

    fn parse_json<T: DeserializeOwned>(str: &'static str) -> T {
        serde_json::de::from_str(str).unwrap()
    }

    pub(crate) fn lookup_avatar_name(&self, avatar_id: u32) -> Option<String> {
        if avatar_id == 0 {
            return None;
        }

        if avatar_id >= 8000 {
            Some("Trailblazer".to_owned())
        } else {
            let cfg = self.avatar_config.get(&avatar_id)?;
            cfg.AvatarName.lookup(&self.text_map).map(|s| s.to_string())
        }
    }
}
