`src/export/fribbels/converters.rs`:

```rs
use reliquary::network::command::proto::AvatarPathData::AvatarPathData as ProtoAvatarPathData;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::AvatarPathSkillTree::AvatarPathSkillTree as ProtoSkillTree;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::Equipment::Equipment as ProtoLightCone;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::Material::Material as ProtoMaterial;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::Relic::Relic as ProtoRelic;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::RelicAffix::RelicAffix;
use tracing::{debug, info_span, instrument, trace, warn};

use crate::export::database::Database;
use crate::export::fribbels::models::*;
use crate::export::fribbels::utils::*;

/// Converts a proto material to an export material
#[instrument(name = "material", skip_all, fields(id = proto.tid))]
pub fn export_proto_material(db: &Database, proto: &ProtoMaterial) -> Option<Material> {
    let cfg = db.item_config.get(&proto.tid)?;
    let id = cfg.ID;
    let name = cfg.ItemName.and_then(|hash| db.text_map.get(&hash)).map(|s| s.to_string())?;
    let count = proto.num;

    debug!(material = name, count, "detected");

    Some(Material {
        id,
        name,
        count,
        expire_time: None,
    })
}

/// Converts a proto relic to an export relic
#[tracing::instrument(name = "relic", skip_all, fields(id = proto.tid))]
pub fn export_proto_relic(db: &Database, proto: &ProtoRelic) -> Option<Relic> {
    let Some(relic_config) = db.relic_config.get(&proto.tid) else {
        warn!("unknown relic, skipping");
        return None;
    };

    let set_id = relic_config.SetID;
    let Some(set_config) = db.relic_set_config.get(&set_id) else {
        warn!(set_id, "unknown relic set, skipping relic");
        return None;
    };
    let Some(main_affix_config) = db.relic_main_affix_config.get(&relic_config.MainAffixGroup, &proto.main_affix_id) else {
        warn!(main_affix_id = proto.main_affix_id, "unknown main affix, skipping relic");
        return None;
    };

    let id = proto.unique_id;
    let level = proto.level;
    let lock = proto.is_protected;
    let discard = proto.is_discarded;
    let set_name = set_config
        .SetName
        .lookup(&db.text_map)
        .map(|s| s.to_string())
        .unwrap_or("".to_string());

    let slot = slot_type_to_export(&relic_config.Type)?;
    let rarity = relic_config.MaxLevel / 3;
    let mainstat = main_stat_to_export(&main_affix_config.Property)?.to_string();
    let location = format_location(proto.equip_avatar_id);

    debug!(rarity, set_name, slot, mainstat, location, "detected");

    let export_substats =
        |affixes: &[RelicAffix]| -> Vec<Substat> { affixes.iter().filter_map(|substat| export_substat(db, rarity, substat)).collect() };

    let substats = export_substats(&proto.sub_affix_list);
    let reroll_substats = (!proto.reforge_sub_affix_list.is_empty()).then(|| export_substats(&proto.reforge_sub_affix_list));
    let preview_substats = (!proto.preview_sub_affix_list.is_empty()).then(|| export_substats(&proto.preview_sub_affix_list));

    Some(Relic {
        set_id,
        name: set_name,
        slot,
        rarity,
        level,
        mainstat,
        substats,
        reroll_substats,
        preview_substats,
        location,
        lock,
        discard,
        _uid: id,
    })
}

/// Converts a relic substat to an export substat
#[tracing::instrument(name = "substat", skip_all)]
pub fn export_substat(db: &Database, rarity: u32, substat: &RelicAffix) -> Option<Substat> {
    let Some(cfg) = db.relic_sub_affix_config.get(&rarity, &substat.affix_id) else {
        // The relic is still exported, just without this substat, so this one matters most
        warn!(rarity, affix_id = substat.affix_id, "unknown substat, leaving it out of the relic");
        return None;
    };
    let key = sub_stat_to_export(&cfg.Property)?.to_string();

    let mut value = substat.cnt as f32 * *cfg.BaseValue + substat.step as f32 * *cfg.StepValue;

    if key.ends_with('_') {
        value *= 100.0;
    }

    trace!(key, value, "detected substat");

    Some(Substat {
        key,
        value,
        count: substat.cnt,
        step: substat.step,
    })
}

/// Converts a proto light cone to an export light cone
#[instrument(name = "light_cone", skip_all, fields(id = proto.tid))]
pub fn export_proto_light_cone(db: &Database, proto: &ProtoLightCone) -> Option<LightCone> {
    let Some(cfg) = db.equipment_config.get(&proto.tid) else {
        warn!("unknown light cone, skipping");
        return None;
    };
    let id = cfg.EquipmentID;
    let name = cfg.EquipmentName.lookup(&db.text_map).map(|s| s.to_string())?;

    let level = proto.level;
    let superimposition = proto.rank;

    debug!(light_cone = name, level, superimposition, "detected");

    let location = format_location(proto.equip_avatar_id);

    Some(LightCone {
        id,
        name,
        level,
        ascension: proto.promotion,
        superimposition,
        location,
        lock: proto.is_protected,
        _uid: proto.unique_id,
    })
}

/// Converts a proto multipath character to an export character
pub fn export_proto_multipath_character(db: &Database, proto: &ProtoAvatarPathData) -> Option<Character> {
    let id = proto.avatar_id;
    let name = db.lookup_avatar_name(id)?;
    let path = avatar_path_lookup(db, id)?.to_owned();

    let span = info_span!("character", name, path);
    let _enter = span.enter();

    trace!(character = name, path, "detected");

    let (skills, traces, memosprite) = export_skill_tree(&proto.avatar_path_skill_tree);
    let ability_version = proto.skilltree_version;

    Some(Character {
        id,
        name,
        path,
        // Level and ascension are stored in the base avatar
        // in the main character list, set them to 0 for now.
        // Will be updated in [resolve_multipath_character]
        level: 0,
        ascension: 0,
        eidolon: proto.rank,
        skills,
        traces,
        memosprite,
        ability_version,
    })
}

/// Extracts skills, traces, and memosprite from a skill tree
pub fn export_skill_tree(proto: &[ProtoSkillTree]) -> (Skills, Traces, Option<Memosprite>) {
    let mut skills = Skills::default();
    let mut traces = Traces::default();
    let mut memosprite = Memosprite::default();

    for skill in proto.iter().filter(|s| s.point_id != 0) {
        let level = skill.level;

        let span = info_span!("skill", id = skill.point_id, level);
        let _enter = span.enter();

        match skill.point_id {
            1 => {
                trace!(level, "detected basic atk trace");
                skills.basic = level;
            }
            2 => {
                trace!(level, "detected skill trace");
                skills.skill = level;
            }
            3 => {
                trace!(level, "detected ult trace");
                skills.ult = level;
            }
            4 => {
                trace!(level, "detected talent trace");
                skills.talent = level;
            }

            5 => {
                trace!(level, "detected technique trace");
                /* technique */
            }

            6 => {
                trace!("detected major trace 1");
                traces.ability_1 = true;
            }
            7 => {
                trace!("detected major trace 2");
                traces.ability_2 = true;
            }
            8 => {
                trace!("detected major trace 3");
                traces.ability_3 = true;
            }

            9 => {
                trace!("detected minor trace 1");
                traces.stat_1 = true;
            }
            10 => {
                trace!("detected minor trace 2");
                traces.stat_2 = true;
            }
            11 => {
                trace!("detected minor trace 3");
                traces.stat_3 = true;
            }
            12 => {
                trace!("detected minor trace 4");
                traces.stat_4 = true;
            }
            13 => {
                trace!("detected minor trace 5");
                traces.stat_5 = true;
            }
            14 => {
                trace!("detected minor trace 6");
                traces.stat_6 = true;
            }
            15 => {
                trace!("detected minor trace 7");
                traces.stat_7 = true;
            }
            16 => {
                trace!("detected minor trace 8");
                traces.stat_8 = true;
            }
            17 => {
                trace!("detected minor trace 9");
                traces.stat_9 = true;
            }
            18 => {
                trace!("detected minor trace 10");
                traces.stat_10 = true;
            }
            19 => {
                trace!("detected memosprite skill trace");
                memosprite.skill = level;
            }
            20 => {
                trace!("detected memosprite talent trace");
                memosprite.talent = level;
            }
            21 => {
                trace!("detected special trace",);
                traces.special = true;
            }
            22 => {
                trace!("detected elation skill trace");
                skills.elation = Some(level);
            }

            _ => {
                warn!(anchor = skill.point_id, "unknown point anchor");
                continue;
            }
        }
    }

    (skills, traces, memosprite.if_present())
}

```

`src/export/fribbels/exporter.rs`:

```rs
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, HashMap, HashSet};

use reliquary::network::command::proto::Avatar::Avatar as ProtoCharacter;
use reliquary::network::command::{GameCommand, command_id};
#[cfg(feature = "stream")]
use tokio::sync::broadcast;
use tracing::{debug, info, instrument, trace, warn};

use crate::export::Exporter;
use crate::export::database::{Database, get_database};
use crate::export::fribbels::models::*;

pub struct OptimizerExporter {
    pub(super) database: &'static Database,

    // State fields
    pub(super) initialized: bool,
    pub(super) uid: Option<u32>,
    pub(super) trailblazer: Option<&'static str>,
    pub(super) banners: HashMap<u32, BannerInfo>,
    pub(super) gacha: GachaFunds,
    pub(super) materials: BTreeMap<u32, Material>,
    pub(super) light_cones: BTreeMap<u32, LightCone>,
    pub(super) relics: BTreeMap<u32, Relic>,
    /// All known characters. Level and ascension are only filled in once the base avatar is known
    pub(super) characters: BTreeMap<u32, Character>,
    pub(super) multipath_base_avatars: HashMap<u32, ProtoCharacter>,
    /// Characters in `characters` that are still waiting for their base avatar
    pub(super) unresolved_multipath_characters: HashSet<u32>,

    #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
    pub(super) event_channel: broadcast::Sender<OptimizerEvent>,
}

impl Default for OptimizerExporter {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl OptimizerExporter {
    pub fn new() -> OptimizerExporter {
        OptimizerExporter {
            database: get_database(),

            // Initialize state fields
            initialized: false,
            uid: None,
            trailblazer: None,
            banners: HashMap::new(),
            gacha: GachaFunds::default(),
            materials: BTreeMap::new(),
            light_cones: BTreeMap::new(),
            relics: BTreeMap::new(),
            characters: BTreeMap::new(),
            multipath_base_avatars: HashMap::new(),
            unresolved_multipath_characters: HashSet::new(),

            #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
            event_channel: broadcast::channel(16).0,
        }
    }

    /// The uid of the account, once it is known
    pub fn uid(&self) -> Option<u32> {
        self.uid
    }

    /// Which trailblazer the account plays, once it is known
    pub fn trailblazer(&self) -> Option<&'static str> {
        self.trailblazer
    }

    pub fn gacha_funds(&self) -> GachaFunds {
        self.gacha
    }

    /// The gacha banners by id
    pub fn banners(&self) -> &HashMap<u32, BannerInfo> {
        &self.banners
    }

    /// Materials by id
    pub fn materials(&self) -> &BTreeMap<u32, Material> {
        &self.materials
    }

    /// Light cones by unique id
    pub fn light_cones(&self) -> &BTreeMap<u32, LightCone> {
        &self.light_cones
    }

    /// Relics by unique id
    pub fn relics(&self) -> &BTreeMap<u32, Relic> {
        &self.relics
    }

    /// Characters by id.
    ///
    /// A multipath character's level and ascension come from its base avatar. Until that has been
    /// captured, they are 0, like in the export.
    pub fn characters(&self) -> &BTreeMap<u32, Character> {
        &self.characters
    }

    #[allow(unused_variables)]
    fn emit_event(&self, event: OptimizerEvent) {
        if self.initialized {
            // Send only fails if there are no active receivers. We don't care if this is the case.
            #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
            self.event_channel.send(event).ok();
        } else {
            // Don't start sending real-time updates until we've completed initialization.
        }
    }

    fn reset(&mut self) {
        self.initialized = false;
        self.uid = None;
        self.trailblazer = None;
        self.banners.clear();
        self.gacha = GachaFunds::default();
        self.materials.clear();
        self.light_cones.clear();
        self.relics.clear();
        self.characters.clear();
        self.multipath_base_avatars.clear();
        self.unresolved_multipath_characters.clear();
    }

    fn is_finishable(&self) -> bool {
        self.trailblazer.is_some()
            && self.uid.is_some()
            && !self.relics.is_empty()
            // at least one character has its level and ascension
            && self.characters.len() > self.unresolved_multipath_characters.len()
            && !self.light_cones.is_empty()
    }

    fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.trailblazer.is_none()
            && self.uid.is_none()
            && self.relics.is_empty()
            && self.characters.is_empty()
            && self.light_cones.is_empty()
    }

    #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
    fn emit_initial_scan(&self) {
        let export = self.export().expect("initial scan failed");
        self.emit_event(OptimizerEvent::InitialScan(export));
    }
}

/// Parses the payload of a command, logging if it doesn't have the expected shape
fn parse<T: protobuf::Message>(command: &GameCommand, description: &str) -> Option<T> {
    command
        .parse_proto()
        .inspect_err(|error| warn!(%error, "could not parse {description} command"))
        .ok()
}

impl Exporter for OptimizerExporter {
    type Export = Export;

    #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
    type LiveEvent = OptimizerEvent;

    fn read_command(&mut self, command: GameCommand) {
        match command.command_id {
            command_id::PlayerGetTokenScRsp => {
                info!("detected new login attempt, resetting state");
                self.reset();

                debug!("detected uid");
                if let Some(cmd) = parse(&command, "token") {
                    self.handle_token(cmd);
                }
            }
            command_id::PlayerLoginScRsp => {
                debug!("detected login info packet");
                if let Some(cmd) = parse(&command, "login info data") {
                    self.handle_login(cmd);
                }
            }
            command_id::GetBagScRsp => {
                debug!("detected inventory packet");
                if let Some(cmd) = parse(&command, "inventory data") {
                    self.handle_inventory(cmd);
                }
            }
            command_id::GetAvatarDataScRsp => {
                debug!("detected character packet");
                if let Some(cmd) = parse(&command, "character data") {
                    self.handle_characters(cmd);
                }
            }
            command_id::SetAvatarEnhancedIdScRsp => {
                debug!("detected set avatar enhanced packet");
                if let Some(event) = parse(&command, "set avatar enhanced data").and_then(|cmd| self.handle_set_avatar_enhanced(cmd)) {
                    self.emit_event(event);
                }
            }
            command_id::GetGachaInfoScRsp => {
                debug!("detected gacha info packet");
                if let Some(cmd) = parse(&command, "gacha info data") {
                    self.handle_gacha_info(cmd);
                }
            }
            command_id::DoGachaScRsp => {
                debug!("detected gacha packet");
                if let Some(event) = parse(&command, "gacha data").and_then(|cmd| self.handle_gacha(cmd)) {
                    self.emit_event(event);
                }
            }
            command_id::PlayerSyncScNotify => {
                debug!("detected player sync packet");
                if let Some(cmd) = parse(&command, "player sync data") {
                    for event in self.handle_player_sync(cmd) {
                        self.emit_event(event);
                    }
                }
            }
            _ => {
                trace!(command_id = command.command_id, tag = command.get_command_name(), "ignored");
            }
        }

        if !self.initialized && self.is_finishable() {
            self.initialized = true;
            info!("finished initialization");

            #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
            self.emit_initial_scan();
        }
    }

    fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.is_empty()
    }

    fn is_initialized(&self) -> bool {
        self.initialized
    }

    #[instrument(skip_all)]
    fn export(&self) -> Option<Self::Export> {
        if self.is_empty() {
            warn!("no data was recorded");
            return None;
        }

        if self.trailblazer.is_none() {
            warn!("trailblazer gender was not recorded");
        }

        if self.uid.is_none() {
            warn!("uid was not recorded");
        }

        if self.relics.is_empty() {
            warn!("relics were not recorded");
        }

        if self.light_cones.is_empty() {
            warn!("light cones were not recorded");
        }

        if self.characters.is_empty() {
            warn!("characters were not recorded");
        }

        if !self.unresolved_multipath_characters.is_empty() {
            warn!(
                num = self.unresolved_multipath_characters.len(),
                "multipath characters were not resolved"
            );
        }

        let export = Export {
            source: "reliquary_archiver",
            build: env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"),
            version: 4,
            metadata: Metadata {
                uid: self.uid,
                trailblazer: self.trailblazer,
            },
            gacha: self.gacha,
            materials: self.materials.values().cloned().collect(),
            light_cones: self.light_cones.values().cloned().collect(),
            relics: self.relics.values().cloned().collect(),
            characters: self.characters.values().cloned().collect(),
        };

        Some(export)
    }

    #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
    fn subscribe(&self) -> (Option<OptimizerEvent>, broadcast::Receiver<OptimizerEvent>) {
        (
            if self.is_initialized() {
                Some(OptimizerEvent::InitialScan(
                    self.export().expect("marked as initialized but data was not recorded"),
                ))
            } else {
                None
            },
            self.event_channel.subscribe(),
        )
    }
}

```

`src/export/fribbels/handlers.rs`:

```rs
use reliquary::network::command::proto::Avatar::Avatar as ProtoCharacter;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::AvatarPathData::AvatarPathData;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::DoGachaScRsp::DoGachaScRsp;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::Equipment::Equipment as ProtoLightCone;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::GetAvatarDataScRsp::GetAvatarDataScRsp;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::GetBagScRsp::GetBagScRsp;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::GetGachaInfoScRsp::GetGachaInfoScRsp;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::Material::Material as ProtoMaterial;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::PlayerGetTokenScRsp::PlayerGetTokenScRsp;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::PlayerLoginScRsp::PlayerLoginScRsp;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::PlayerSyncScNotify::PlayerSyncScNotify;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::Relic::Relic as ProtoRelic;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::SetAvatarEnhancedIdScRsp::SetAvatarEnhancedIdScRsp;
use tracing::{debug, info, warn};

use super::OptimizerExporter;
use crate::export::fribbels::converters::*;
use crate::export::fribbels::models::*;

impl OptimizerExporter {
    pub(super) fn handle_token(&mut self, token: PlayerGetTokenScRsp) {
        self.uid = Some(token.uid);
    }

    pub(super) fn handle_login(&mut self, login: PlayerLoginScRsp) {
        self.gacha.oneric_shards = login.basic_info.oneric_shard_count;
        self.gacha.stellar_jade = login.basic_info.stellar_jade_count;
    }

    /// Converts the items and stores them, returning what was stored
    fn ingest_items(
        &mut self,
        relics: &[ProtoRelic],
        light_cones: &[ProtoLightCone],
        materials: &[ProtoMaterial],
    ) -> (Vec<Relic>, Vec<LightCone>, Vec<Material>) {
        let relics: Vec<Relic> = relics.iter().filter_map(|r| export_proto_relic(self.database, r)).collect();
        for relic in &relics {
            self.relics.insert(relic._uid, relic.clone());
        }

        let light_cones: Vec<LightCone> = light_cones
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|equip| export_proto_light_cone(self.database, equip))
            .collect();
        for light_cone in &light_cones {
            self.light_cones.insert(light_cone._uid, light_cone.clone());
        }

        let materials: Vec<Material> = materials.iter().filter_map(|m| export_proto_material(self.database, m)).collect();
        for material in &materials {
            self.materials.insert(material.id, material.clone());
        }

        (relics, light_cones, materials)
    }

    pub(super) fn handle_inventory(&mut self, bag: GetBagScRsp) {
        let (relics, light_cones, materials) = self.ingest_items(&bag.relic_list, &bag.equipment_list, &bag.material_list);

        info!(num = relics.len(), "found relics");
        info!(num = light_cones.len(), "found light cones");
        info!(num = materials.len(), "found materials");
    }

    pub(super) fn ingest_character(&mut self, proto_character: &ProtoCharacter) {
        self.multipath_base_avatars
            .insert(proto_character.base_avatar_id, proto_character.clone());

        // Try to resolve any multipath characters that have this as their base avatar.
        for unresolved_avatar_id in self.unresolved_multipath_characters.clone().iter() {
            self.resolve_multipath_character(*unresolved_avatar_id);
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn ingest_multipath_character(&mut self, proto_multipath_character: &AvatarPathData) -> Option<Character> {
        let Some(character) = export_proto_multipath_character(self.database, proto_multipath_character) else {
            warn!(
                uid = &proto_multipath_character.avatar_id,
                "multipath character config not found, skipping"
            );
            return None;
        };

        let character_id = character.id;

        // If it's the trailblazer, determine the gender
        if character.name == "Trailblazer" {
            self.trailblazer = Some(if character_id % 2 == 0 { "Stelle" } else { "Caelus" });
        }

        self.characters.insert(character_id, character);

        let resolved = self.resolve_multipath_character(character_id);
        if resolved.is_none() {
            debug!(uid = &character_id, "multipath character not resolved");
            self.unresolved_multipath_characters.insert(character_id);
        }

        resolved
    }

    pub(super) fn handle_characters(&mut self, characters: GetAvatarDataScRsp) {
        info!(num = characters.avatar_list.len(), "found characters");
        for character in characters.avatar_list {
            self.ingest_character(&character);
        }

        info!(num = characters.avatar_path_data_info_list.len(), "found multipath characters");
        for multipath_avatar_info in characters.avatar_path_data_info_list {
            self.ingest_multipath_character(&multipath_avatar_info);
        }
    }

    fn get_multipath_base_id(&self, avatar_id: u32) -> u32 {
        self.database
            .multipath_avatar_config
            .get(&avatar_id)
            .map(|cfg| cfg.BaseAvatarID)
            .unwrap_or(avatar_id)
    }

    /// Fills in the level and ascension of a character, which are stored in its base avatar
    pub(super) fn resolve_multipath_character(&mut self, character_id: u32) -> Option<Character> {
        let base_avatar_id = self.get_multipath_base_id(character_id);
        let Some(character) = self.characters.get_mut(&character_id) else {
            warn!(uid = &character_id, "multipath character not found");
            return None;
        };

        let base_avatar = self.multipath_base_avatars.get(&base_avatar_id)?;
        character.level = base_avatar.level;
        character.ascension = base_avatar.promotion;

        self.unresolved_multipath_characters.remove(&character_id);

        Some(character.clone())
    }

    pub(super) fn handle_player_sync(&mut self, sync: PlayerSyncScNotify) -> Vec<OptimizerEvent> {
        let mut events = Vec::new();

        let (relics, light_cones, materials) = self.ingest_items(&sync.relic_list, &sync.equipment_list, &sync.material_list);

        if !relics.is_empty() {
            info!(num = relics.len(), "found updated relics");
            events.push(OptimizerEvent::UpdateRelics(relics));
        }

        if !light_cones.is_empty() {
            info!(num = light_cones.len(), "found updated light cones");
            events.push(OptimizerEvent::UpdateLightCones(light_cones));
        }

        if !materials.is_empty() {
            info!(num = materials.len(), "found updated materials");
            events.push(OptimizerEvent::UpdateMaterials(materials));
        }

        if let Some(basic_info) = sync.basic_info.into_option() {
            self.gacha.oneric_shards = basic_info.oneric_shard_count;
            self.gacha.stellar_jade = basic_info.stellar_jade_count;

            events.push(OptimizerEvent::UpdateGachaFunds(self.gacha));
        }

        if !sync.del_relic_list.is_empty() {
            info!(num = sync.del_relic_list.len(), "found deleted relics");
            for del_relic in sync.del_relic_list.iter() {
                if self.relics.remove(del_relic).is_none() {
                    warn!(uid = &del_relic, "del_relic not found");
                }
            }

            events.push(OptimizerEvent::DeleteRelics(sync.del_relic_list));
        }

        if !sync.del_equipment_list.is_empty() {
            info!(num = sync.del_equipment_list.len(), "found deleted light cones");
            for del_light_cone in sync.del_equipment_list.iter() {
                if self.light_cones.remove(del_light_cone).is_none() {
                    warn!(uid = &del_light_cone, "del_light_cone not found");
                }
            }

            events.push(OptimizerEvent::DeleteLightCones(sync.del_equipment_list));
        }

        let mut updated_characters = Vec::new();

        if let Some(avatar_sync) = sync.avatar_sync.into_option() {
            for avatar in avatar_sync.avatar_list {
                self.ingest_character(&avatar)
            }

            for multipath_avatar_info in avatar_sync.avatar_path_data_info_list {
                if let Some(character) = self.ingest_multipath_character(&multipath_avatar_info) {
                    updated_characters.push(character);
                } else {
                    warn!(uid = &multipath_avatar_info.avatar_id, "multipath character not resolved");
                }
            }
        }

        if !updated_characters.is_empty() {
            info!(num = updated_characters.len(), "found updated characters");
            events.push(OptimizerEvent::UpdateCharacters(updated_characters));
        }

        events
    }

    pub(super) fn handle_set_avatar_enhanced(&mut self, set_avatar_enhanced: SetAvatarEnhancedIdScRsp) -> Option<OptimizerEvent> {
        let Some(character) = self.characters.get_mut(&set_avatar_enhanced.growth_avatar_id) else {
            warn!(
                uid = &set_avatar_enhanced.growth_avatar_id,
                "character not found when setting enhanced id, skipping"
            );
            return None;
        };

        character.ability_version = set_avatar_enhanced.skilltree_version;

        Some(OptimizerEvent::UpdateCharacters(vec![character.clone()]))
    }

    fn is_lightcone(&self, item_id: u32) -> bool {
        self.database.equipment_config.get(&item_id).is_some()
    }

    /// The star rating of a light cone or character that can be pulled from a banner
    fn gacha_item_grade(&self, item_id: u32) -> Option<u32> {
        let rarity = if let Some(lc_config) = self.database.equipment_config.get(&item_id) {
            &lc_config.Rarity
        } else {
            &self.database.avatar_config.get(&item_id)?.Rarity
        };

        match rarity.as_str() {
            "CombatPowerLightconeRarity5" | "CombatPowerAvatarRarityType5" => Some(5),
            "CombatPowerLightconeRarity4" | "CombatPowerAvatarRarityType4" => Some(4),
            "CombatPowerLightconeRarity3" => Some(3),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn handle_gacha_info(&mut self, gacha_info: GetGachaInfoScRsp) {
        for banner in gacha_info.gacha_info_list {
            let banner_type = match banner.gacha_id {
                1001 => BannerType::Standard,
                _ if banner.prize_item_list.first().is_some_and(|item_id| self.is_lightcone(*item_id)) => BannerType::LightCone,
                _ => BannerType::Character,
            };

            self.banners.insert(
                banner.gacha_id,
                BannerInfo {
                    rate_up_item_list: banner.item_detail_list,
                    banner_type,
                },
            );
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn handle_gacha(&mut self, gacha: DoGachaScRsp) -> Option<OptimizerEvent> {
        let Some(banner) = self.banners.get(&gacha.gacha_id) else {
            warn!(gacha_id = &gacha.gacha_id, "gacha info not found");
            return None;
        };

        let mut gacha_result = GachaResult {
            banner_id: gacha.gacha_id,
            banner_type: banner.banner_type,
            pity_4: PityUpdate::AddPity { amount: 0 },
            pity_5: PityUpdate::AddPity { amount: 0 },
            pull_results: Vec::new(),
        };

        for item in gacha.gacha_item_list {
            let item_id = item.gacha_item.item_id;
            gacha_result.pull_results.push(item_id);

            // Guessing the rarity would report a wrong pity, which is worse than not reporting the pull
            let Some(grade) = self.gacha_item_grade(item_id) else {
                warn!(item_id, "pulled an item of unknown rarity, skipping gacha result");
                return None;
            };

            let was_rate_up = banner.rate_up_item_list.contains(&item_id);
            let next_is_guarantee = !was_rate_up;

            match grade {
                5 => {
                    gacha_result.pity_4.increment();
                    gacha_result.pity_5.reset(next_is_guarantee);
                }
                4 => {
                    gacha_result.pity_4.reset(next_is_guarantee);
                    gacha_result.pity_5.increment();
                }
                _ => {
                    gacha_result.pity_4.increment();
                    gacha_result.pity_5.increment();
                }
            }
        }

        Some(OptimizerEvent::GachaResult(gacha_result))
    }
}

```

`src/export/fribbels/models.rs`:

```rs
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_with::{DisplayFromStr, serde_as};

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Export {
    pub source: &'static str,
    pub build: &'static str,
    pub version: u32,
    pub metadata: Metadata,
    pub gacha: GachaFunds,
    pub materials: Vec<Material>,
    pub light_cones: Vec<LightCone>,
    pub relics: Vec<Relic>,
    pub characters: Vec<Character>,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Metadata {
    pub uid: Option<u32>,
    pub trailblazer: Option<&'static str>,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]
pub struct GachaFunds {
    pub stellar_jade: u32,
    pub oneric_shards: u32,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub enum BannerType {
    Character,
    LightCone,
    Standard,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct BannerInfo {
    pub rate_up_item_list: Vec<u32>,
    pub banner_type: BannerType,
}

#[serde_as]
#[derive(Serialize, Debug, Clone)]
#[serde(tag = "event", content = "data")]
pub enum OptimizerEvent {
    InitialScan(Export),
    GachaResult(GachaResult),
    UpdateGachaFunds(GachaFunds),
    UpdateMaterials(Vec<Material>),
    UpdateLightCones(Vec<LightCone>),
    UpdateRelics(Vec<Relic>),
    UpdateCharacters(Vec<Character>),
    DeleteRelics(#[serde_as(as = "Vec<DisplayFromStr>")] Vec<u32>),
    DeleteLightCones(#[serde_as(as = "Vec<DisplayFromStr>")] Vec<u32>),
}

#[serde_as]
#[derive(Serialize, Debug, Clone)]
pub struct GachaResult {
    #[serde_as(as = "DisplayFromStr")]
    pub banner_id: u32,
    pub banner_type: BannerType,
    pub pity_4: PityUpdate,
    pub pity_5: PityUpdate,
    #[serde_as(as = "Vec<DisplayFromStr>")]
    pub pull_results: Vec<u32>,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Debug, Clone, Copy)]
#[serde(tag = "kind")]
pub enum PityUpdate {
    AddPity { amount: u32 },
    ResetPity { amount: u32, set_guarantee: bool },
}

impl PityUpdate {
    pub fn increment(&mut self) {
        match self {
            PityUpdate::AddPity { amount, .. } => *amount += 1,
            PityUpdate::ResetPity { amount, .. } => *amount += 1,
        }
    }

    pub fn reset(&mut self, guarantee: bool) {
        *self = PityUpdate::ResetPity {
            amount: 0,
            set_guarantee: guarantee,
        }
    }
}

#[serde_as]
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct Material {
    #[serde_as(as = "DisplayFromStr")]
    pub id: u32,
    pub name: String,
    pub count: u32,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub expire_time: Option<u64>,
}

#[serde_as]
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct Relic {
    #[serde_as(as = "DisplayFromStr")]
    pub set_id: u32,
    pub name: String,
    pub slot: &'static str,
    pub rarity: u32,
    pub level: u32,
    pub mainstat: String,
    pub substats: Vec<Substat>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub reroll_substats: Option<Vec<Substat>>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub preview_substats: Option<Vec<Substat>>,
    pub location: String,
    pub lock: bool,
    pub discard: bool,
    #[serde_as(as = "DisplayFromStr")]
    pub _uid: u32,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct Substat {
    pub key: String,
    pub value: f32,
    pub count: u32,
    pub step: u32,
}

#[serde_as]
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct LightCone {
    #[serde_as(as = "DisplayFromStr")]
    pub id: u32,
    pub name: String,
    pub level: u32,
    pub ascension: u32,
    pub superimposition: u32,
    pub location: String,
    pub lock: bool,
    #[serde_as(as = "DisplayFromStr")]
    pub _uid: u32,
}

#[serde_as]
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct Character {
    #[serde_as(as = "DisplayFromStr")]
    pub id: u32,
    pub name: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub level: u32,
    pub ascension: u32,
    pub eidolon: u32,
    pub skills: Skills,
    pub traces: Traces,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub memosprite: Option<Memosprite>,
    pub ability_version: u32,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct Skills {
    pub basic: u32,
    pub skill: u32,
    pub ult: u32,
    pub talent: u32,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub elation: Option<u32>,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct Traces {
    pub ability_1: bool,
    pub ability_2: bool,
    pub ability_3: bool,
    pub stat_1: bool,
    pub stat_2: bool,
    pub stat_3: bool,
    pub stat_4: bool,
    pub stat_5: bool,
    pub stat_6: bool,
    pub stat_7: bool,
    pub stat_8: bool,
    pub stat_9: bool,
    pub stat_10: bool,
    pub special: bool,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct Memosprite {
    pub skill: u32,
    pub talent: u32,
}

impl Memosprite {
    pub fn if_present(self) -> Option<Memosprite> {
        if self.skill == 0 && self.talent == 0 { None } else { Some(self) }
    }
}
```

`src/export/fribbels/tests.rs`:

```rs
use reliquary::network::command::proto::Avatar::Avatar as ProtoCharacter;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::AvatarPathData::AvatarPathData;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::AvatarPathSkillTree::AvatarPathSkillTree;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::DoGachaScRsp::DoGachaScRsp;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::GachaInfo::GachaInfo;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::GachaItem::GachaItem;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::GetGachaInfoScRsp::GetGachaInfoScRsp;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::Item::Item;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::PlayerSyncScNotify::PlayerSyncScNotify;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::Relic::Relic as ProtoRelic;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::RelicAffix::RelicAffix;
use reliquary::network::command::proto::SetAvatarEnhancedIdScRsp::SetAvatarEnhancedIdScRsp;

use super::converters::*;
use super::utils::*;
use super::*;
use crate::export::Exporter;
use crate::export::database::get_database;

// Long-standing game data that the embedded database is expected to contain
const MARCH_7TH: u32 = 1001; // 4* character
const HIMEKO: u32 = 1003; // 5* character
const ARROWS: u32 = 20000; // 3* light cone
const TWO_STAR_HEAD: u32 = 31011; // relic of set 101
const CHARACTER_BANNER: u32 = 2001;

fn relic(unique_id: u32, main_affix_id: u32) -> ProtoRelic {
    ProtoRelic {
        unique_id,
        tid: TWO_STAR_HEAD,
        main_affix_id,
        sub_affix_list: vec![RelicAffix {
            affix_id: 1,
            cnt: 2,
            step: 1,
            ..Default::default()
        }],
        ..Default::default()
    }
}

fn path_data(avatar_id: u32) -> AvatarPathData {
    AvatarPathData {
        avatar_id,
        rank: 2,
        ..Default::default()
    }
}

fn base_avatar(base_avatar_id: u32, level: u32, promotion: u32) -> ProtoCharacter {
    ProtoCharacter {
        base_avatar_id,
        level,
        promotion,
        ..Default::default()
    }
}

fn pull(item_ids: &[u32]) -> DoGachaScRsp {
    DoGachaScRsp {
        gacha_id: CHARACTER_BANNER,
        gacha_item_list: item_ids
            .iter()
            .map(|&item_id| GachaItem {
                gacha_item: Some(Item {
                    item_id,
                    ..Default::default()
                })
                .into(),
                ..Default::default()
            })
            .collect(),
        ..Default::default()
    }
}

fn exporter_with_banner(rate_up: u32) -> OptimizerExporter {
    let mut exporter = OptimizerExporter::new();
    exporter.handle_gacha_info(GetGachaInfoScRsp {
        gacha_info_list: vec![GachaInfo {
            gacha_id: CHARACTER_BANNER,
            prize_item_list: vec![rate_up],
            item_detail_list: vec![rate_up],
            ..Default::default()
        }],
        ..Default::default()
    });
    exporter
}

#[test]
fn unknown_game_strings_are_not_mapped() {
    assert_eq!(slot_type_to_export("HEAD"), Some("Head"));
    assert_eq!(slot_type_to_export("TAIL"), None);
    assert_eq!(main_stat_to_export("SpeedDelta"), Some("SPD"));
    assert_eq!(main_stat_to_export("LuckDelta"), None);
    assert_eq!(sub_stat_to_export("HPAddedRatio"), Some("HP_"));
    assert_eq!(sub_stat_to_export("LuckDelta"), None);
}

#[test]
fn relic_is_converted() {
    let relic = export_proto_relic(get_database(), &relic(7, 1)).expect("relic should convert");

    assert_eq!(relic._uid, 7);
    assert_eq!(relic.set_id, 101);
    assert_eq!(relic.slot, "Head");
    assert_eq!(relic.rarity, 2);
    assert_eq!(relic.mainstat, "HP");
    assert_eq!(relic.substats.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(relic.substats[0].key, "HP");
    assert_eq!(relic.substats[0].count, 2);
    assert!(relic.reroll_substats.is_none());
}

#[test]
fn relic_with_unknown_main_affix_is_skipped() {
    assert!(export_proto_relic(get_database(), &relic(7, 9999)).is_none());
}

#[test]
fn skill_tree_points_are_mapped() {
    let point = |point_id, level| AvatarPathSkillTree {
        point_id,
        level,
        ..Default::default()
    };

    let (skills, traces, memosprite) =
        export_skill_tree(&[point(1, 6), point(3, 10), point(6, 1), point(18, 1), point(999, 1), point(0, 5)]);

    assert_eq!((skills.basic, skills.skill, skills.ult, skills.talent), (6, 0, 10, 0));
    assert_eq!(skills.elation, None);
    assert!(traces.ability_1 && traces.stat_10);
    assert!(!traces.ability_2 && !traces.special);
    assert!(memosprite.is_none());

    let (skills, _, memosprite) = export_skill_tree(&[point(19, 3), point(22, 2)]);
    assert_eq!(memosprite.map(|m| (m.skill, m.talent)), Some((3, 0)));
    assert_eq!(skills.elation, Some(2));
}

#[test]
fn character_gets_its_level_once_the_base_avatar_is_known() {
    let mut exporter = OptimizerExporter::new();

    assert!(exporter.ingest_multipath_character(&path_data(MARCH_7TH)).is_none());
    assert_eq!(exporter.characters[&MARCH_7TH].level, 0);

    exporter.ingest_character(&base_avatar(MARCH_7TH, 80, 6));

    let character = &exporter.export().unwrap().characters[0];
    assert_eq!(
        (character.id, character.level, character.ascension, character.eidolon),
        (MARCH_7TH, 80, 6, 2)
    );
}

#[test]
fn character_arriving_after_its_base_avatar_is_resolved_immediately() {
    let mut exporter = OptimizerExporter::new();
    exporter.ingest_character(&base_avatar(MARCH_7TH, 50, 3));

    let character = exporter
        .ingest_multipath_character(&path_data(MARCH_7TH))
        .expect("should be resolved");
    assert_eq!(character.level, 50);
}

#[test]
fn enhanced_ability_version_shows_up_in_export() {
    let mut exporter = OptimizerExporter::new();
    exporter.ingest_character(&base_avatar(MARCH_7TH, 80, 6));
    exporter.ingest_multipath_character(&path_data(MARCH_7TH));

    let event = exporter.handle_set_avatar_enhanced(SetAvatarEnhancedIdScRsp {
        growth_avatar_id: MARCH_7TH,
        skilltree_version: 1,
        ..Default::default()
    });

    assert!(matches!(event, Some(OptimizerEvent::UpdateCharacters(characters)) if characters[0].ability_version == 1));
    assert_eq!(exporter.export().unwrap().characters[0].ability_version, 1);

    let unknown = exporter.handle_set_avatar_enhanced(SetAvatarEnhancedIdScRsp {
        growth_avatar_id: 4242,
        ..Default::default()
    });
    assert!(unknown.is_none());
}

#[test]
fn player_sync_updates_and_deletes_relics() {
    let mut exporter = OptimizerExporter::new();

    let events = exporter.handle_player_sync(PlayerSyncScNotify {
        relic_list: vec![relic(1, 1), relic(2, 1), relic(3, 9999)],
        ..Default::default()
    });
    assert!(matches!(events.as_slice(), [OptimizerEvent::UpdateRelics(relics)] if relics.len() == 2));
    assert_eq!(exporter.relics.len(), 2);

    let events = exporter.handle_player_sync(PlayerSyncScNotify {
        del_relic_list: vec![1],
        ..Default::default()
    });
    assert!(matches!(events.as_slice(), [OptimizerEvent::DeleteRelics(ids)] if ids == &[1]));
    assert_eq!(exporter.relics.keys().copied().collect::<Vec<_>>(), vec![2]);
}

#[test]
fn gacha_pity_follows_the_pulled_rarities() {
    let mut exporter = exporter_with_banner(HIMEKO);

    let Some(OptimizerEvent::GachaResult(result)) = exporter.handle_gacha(pull(&[ARROWS, ARROWS, MARCH_7TH, ARROWS])) else {
        panic!("expected a gacha result");
    };

    assert!(matches!(result.banner_type, BannerType::Character));
    assert_eq!(result.pull_results, vec![ARROWS, ARROWS, MARCH_7TH, ARROWS]);
    assert!(matches!(result.pity_5, PityUpdate::AddPity { amount: 4 }));
    // March 7th is not the rate up, so the next 4* is guaranteed to be one
    assert!(matches!(
        result.pity_4,
        PityUpdate::ResetPity {
            amount: 1,
            set_guarantee: true
        }
    ));

    let Some(OptimizerEvent::GachaResult(result)) = exporter.handle_gacha(pull(&[HIMEKO])) else {
        panic!("expected a gacha result");
    };
    assert!(matches!(
        result.pity_5,
        PityUpdate::ResetPity {
            amount: 0,
            set_guarantee: false
        }
    ));
}

#[test]
fn gacha_result_with_unknown_item_is_dropped() {
    let mut exporter = exporter_with_banner(HIMEKO);
    assert!(exporter.handle_gacha(pull(&[ARROWS, 999_999])).is_none());
}

#[test]
fn gacha_on_unknown_banner_is_dropped() {
    let mut exporter = OptimizerExporter::new();
    assert!(exporter.handle_gacha(pull(&[ARROWS])).is_none());
}

#[test]
fn banner_without_prizes_does_not_panic() {
    let mut exporter = OptimizerExporter::new();
    exporter.handle_gacha_info(GetGachaInfoScRsp {
        gacha_info_list: vec![GachaInfo {
            gacha_id: CHARACTER_BANNER,
            ..Default::default()
        }],
        ..Default::default()
    });
    assert!(matches!(exporter.banners[&CHARACTER_BANNER].banner_type, BannerType::Character));
}
```

`src/export/fribbels/utils.rs`:

```rs
use tracing::{error, warn};

/// Formats character ID as location string for relics and light cones
pub fn format_location(avatar_id: u32) -> String {
    if avatar_id == 0 { "".to_owned() } else { avatar_id.to_string() }
}

/// Converts slot type from game format to export format
pub fn slot_type_to_export(s: &str) -> Option<&'static str> {
    Some(match s {
        "HEAD" => "Head",
        "HAND" => "Hands",
        "BODY" => "Body",
        "FOOT" => "Feet",
        "NECK" => "Planar Sphere",
        "OBJECT" => "Link Rope",
        _ => {
            warn!(slot = s, "unknown relic slot");
            return None;
        }
    })
}

/// Converts main stat property from game format to export format
pub fn main_stat_to_export(s: &str) -> Option<&'static str> {
    Some(match s {
        "HPDelta" => "HP",
        "AttackDelta" => "ATK",
        "HPAddedRatio" => "HP",
        "AttackAddedRatio" => "ATK",
        "DefenceAddedRatio" => "DEF",
        "CriticalChanceBase" => "CRIT Rate",
        "CriticalDamageBase" => "CRIT DMG",
        "HealRatioBase" => "Outgoing Healing Boost",
        "SpeedDelta" => "SPD",
        "StatusProbabilityBase" => "Effect Hit Rate",
        "PhysicalAddedRatio" => "Physical DMG Boost",
        "FireAddedRatio" => "Fire DMG Boost",
        "IceAddedRatio" => "Ice DMG Boost",
        "ThunderAddedRatio" => "Lightning DMG Boost",
        "WindAddedRatio" => "Wind DMG Boost",
        "QuantumAddedRatio" => "Quantum DMG Boost",
        "ImaginaryAddedRatio" => "Imaginary DMG Boost",
        "BreakDamageAddedRatioBase" => "Break Effect",
        "SPRatioBase" => "Energy Regeneration Rate",
        _ => {
            warn!(main_stat = s, "unknown main stat");
            return None;
        }
    })
}

/// Converts sub stat property from game format to export format
pub fn sub_stat_to_export(s: &str) -> Option<&'static str> {
    Some(match s {
        "HPDelta" => "HP",
        "AttackDelta" => "ATK",
        "HPAddedRatio" => "HP_",
        "AttackAddedRatio" => "ATK_",
        "DefenceAddedRatio" => "DEF_",
        "DefenceDelta" => "DEF",
        "CriticalChanceBase" => "CRIT Rate_",
        "CriticalDamageBase" => "CRIT DMG_",
        "SpeedDelta" => "SPD",
        "StatusProbabilityBase" => "Effect Hit Rate_",
        "StatusResistanceBase" => "Effect RES_",
        "BreakDamageAddedRatioBase" => "Break Effect_",
        _ => {
            warn!(sub_stat = s, "unknown sub stat");
            return None;
        }
    })
}

/// Converts avatar base type to path for characters
pub fn avatar_path_lookup(db: &crate::export::database::Database, avatar_id: u32) -> Option<&'static str> {
    let hero_config = db.avatar_config.get(&avatar_id)?;
    let avatar_base_type = hero_config.AvatarBaseType.as_str();
    match avatar_base_type {
        "Knight" => Some("Preservation"),
        "Rogue" => Some("Hunt"),
        "Mage" => Some("Erudition"),
        "Warlock" => Some("Nihility"),
        "Warrior" => Some("Destruction"),
        "Shaman" => Some("Harmony"),
        "Priest" => Some("Abundance"),
        "Memory" => Some("Remembrance"),
        "Elation" => Some("Elation"),
        _ => {
            error!(?avatar_base_type, "unknown path");
            None
        }
    }
}
```

`src/export/mod.rs`:

```rs
use reliquary::network::command::GameCommand;

pub mod database;
pub mod fribbels;

pub trait Exporter: Send + 'static {
    type Export: Send;

    fn read_command(&mut self, command: GameCommand);
    fn is_empty(&self) -> bool;
    fn is_initialized(&self) -> bool;
    fn export(&self) -> Option<Self::Export>;

    #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
    type LiveEvent: serde::Serialize + Send + Clone;

    /// Returns a tuple containing an initial export if the exporter is initialized and a broadcast receiver for live events.
    ///
    /// The first element of the tuple is an optional initial event that represents the current state.
    /// The second element is a broadcast receiver that will receive all future live events.
    ///
    /// This method is only available when the "stream" feature is enabled.
    #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
    fn subscribe(&self) -> (Option<Self::LiveEvent>, tokio::sync::broadcast::Receiver<Self::LiveEvent>);
}
```

`src/logging.rs`:

```rs
use std::any::Any;
use std::fs::File;
use std::io;
use std::io::Write;
use std::path::Path;
#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
use std::sync::MutexGuard;
use std::sync::{Arc, LazyLock, Mutex, OnceLock};

use tracing::info;
use tracing::level_filters::LevelFilter;
use tracing_subscriber::fmt::MakeWriter;
use tracing_subscriber::prelude::*;
use tracing_subscriber::{EnvFilter, Registry, reload};

/// Lines are dropped from the front of the log buffer once it grows past this
const LOG_BUFFER_MAX_LINES: usize = 100000;
/// How many of the most recent lines survive when the log buffer is trimmed
const LOG_BUFFER_KEEP_LINES: usize = 10000;

/// Everything logged to the console, kept around for the GUI's log view and the crash log
static LOG_BUFFER: LazyLock<Mutex<Vec<String>>> = LazyLock::new(|| Mutex::new(Vec::new()));
/// Notified whenever lines are added to [`LOG_BUFFER`]
static LOG_NOTIFY: LazyLock<tokio::sync::Notify> = LazyLock::new(tokio::sync::Notify::new);
/// Changes the filter of the console log, set up by [`tracing_init`]
static CONSOLE_FILTER: OnceLock<reload::Handle<EnvFilter, Registry>> = OnceLock::new();

/// The lines logged to the console so far, oldest first.
///
/// Logging blocks while the guard is held, so don't hold on to it for long.
#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
pub fn lines() -> MutexGuard<'static, Vec<String>> {
    LOG_BUFFER.lock().unwrap()
}

/// Waits until new lines have been logged
#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
pub async fn wait_for_new_lines() {
    LOG_NOTIFY.notified().await;
}

/// What the console logs when nothing more verbose was asked for: the app's own info logs, plus
/// warnings from the libraries that decode and capture traffic. Those warnings (missed packets,
/// KCP errors) are often the only sign of why a capture got stuck, so they belong in screenshots.
///
/// Targets match by prefix and the longest one wins, so `reliquary=warn` doesn't override
/// `reliquary_archiver=info`.
const DEFAULT_DIRECTIVES: &str = "reliquary_archiver=info,reliquary=warn,pktmon=warn";

/// One step up from [`DEFAULT_DIRECTIVES`]. The libraries' info logs include every decoded
/// command and every new conversation, which is what diagnosing a stuck capture needs.
#[cfg(any(feature = "gui", test))]
const DEBUG_DIRECTIVES: &str = "reliquary_archiver=debug,reliquary=info,pktmon=info";

/// The console filter for a level picked in the GUI. The capture libraries stay one level
/// quieter than the app, other crates stay quiet, and trace shows everything.
#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
fn directives_for_level(level: LevelFilter) -> String {
    match level {
        LevelFilter::INFO => DEFAULT_DIRECTIVES.to_string(),
        LevelFilter::DEBUG => DEBUG_DIRECTIVES.to_string(),
        level => level.to_string(),
    }
}

fn console_filter(directives: &str) -> EnvFilter {
    EnvFilter::builder().parse_lossy(directives)
}

/// Changes which levels are logged to the console and the log buffer
#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
pub fn set_console_level(level: LevelFilter) {
    let Some(handle) = CONSOLE_FILTER.get() else {
        return;
    };

    let filter = console_filter(&directives_for_level(level));
    let result = handle.modify(|f| *f = filter);
    if let Err(e) = result {
        tracing::error!("Failed to change log level: {}", e);
    }
}

struct VecWriter;

impl io::Write for VecWriter {
    fn write(&mut self, buf: &[u8]) -> io::Result<usize> {
        let str = String::from_utf8_lossy(buf);
        let lines = str.lines().map(|s| s.to_string());
        {
            let mut buffer = LOG_BUFFER.lock().unwrap();
            buffer.extend(lines);

            // Limit the buffer size to prevent unbounded memory growth
            // (assuming an average of ~100 bytes per log line, the buffer peaks at around 10MB of memory)
            if buffer.len() > LOG_BUFFER_MAX_LINES {
                let range = 0..buffer.len() - LOG_BUFFER_KEEP_LINES;
                buffer.drain(range);
            }
        }
        LOG_NOTIFY.notify_one();
        Ok(buf.len())
    }

    fn flush(&mut self) -> io::Result<()> {
        Ok(())
    }
}

struct DualWriter<A: io::Write, B: io::Write> {
    m: Arc<Mutex<(A, B)>>,
}

impl<A: io::Write, B: io::Write> DualWriter<A, B> {
    fn new(a: A, b: B) -> Self {
        Self {
            m: Arc::new(Mutex::new((a, b))),
        }
    }
}

impl<A: io::Write, B: io::Write> io::Write for DualWriter<A, B> {
    fn write(&mut self, buf: &[u8]) -> io::Result<usize> {
        let mut m = self.m.lock().unwrap();
        m.0.write_all(buf)?;
        m.1.write(buf)
    }

    fn flush(&mut self) -> io::Result<()> {
        let mut m = self.m.lock().unwrap();
        m.0.flush()?;
        m.1.flush()
    }
}

impl<'a, A: io::Write, B: io::Write> MakeWriter<'a> for DualWriter<A, B> {
    type Writer = DualWriter<A, B>;

    fn make_writer(&'a self) -> Self::Writer {
        DualWriter { m: self.m.clone() }
    }
}

pub fn tracing_init(verbose: u8, log_path: Option<&Path>) {
    tracing_log::LogTracer::init().unwrap();

    // RUST_LOG replaces the defaults entirely when it's set
    let directives = std::env::var(EnvFilter::DEFAULT_ENV)
        .ok()
        .filter(|directives| !directives.is_empty())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| {
            match verbose {
                0 => DEFAULT_DIRECTIVES,
                1 => "info",
                2 => "debug",
                _ => "trace",
            }
            .to_string()
        });

    let (filter, handle) = reload::Layer::new(console_filter(&directives));
    let console_log = tracing_subscriber::fmt::layer()
        .with_ansi(false)
        .with_writer(DualWriter::new(VecWriter, io::stdout()))
        .with_filter(filter);

    CONSOLE_FILTER.set(handle).ok();

    let file_log = log_path.map(|log_path| {
        let log_file = File::create(log_path).unwrap();
        tracing_subscriber::fmt::layer()
            .json()
            .with_writer(Mutex::new(log_file))
            .with_filter(LevelFilter::TRACE)
    });

    let subscriber = Registry::default().with(console_log).with(file_log);

    tracing::subscriber::set_global_default(subscriber).expect("unable to set up logging");
}

/// Writes the buffered log and the panic message to crashlog.txt
pub fn write_crashlog(payload: &(dyn Any + Send)) {
    let Ok(mut file) = File::create("crashlog.txt") else {
        return;
    };

    let log = match LOG_BUFFER.try_lock() {
        Ok(buffer) => buffer.join("\n"),
        Err(_) => "failed to lock log buffer".to_string(),
    };

    let panic_message = if let Some(s) = payload.downcast_ref::<&str>() {
        s
    } else if let Some(s) = payload.downcast_ref::<String>() {
        s.as_str()
    } else {
        "panic: unknown payload type"
    };

    if write!(file, "{log}\n\n{panic_message}").is_ok() {
        info!("wrote crashlog to crashlog.txt");
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn default_directives_parse() {
        // parse_lossy drops invalid directives silently, so check them strictly here
        EnvFilter::builder().parse(DEFAULT_DIRECTIVES).unwrap();
        EnvFilter::builder().parse(DEBUG_DIRECTIVES).unwrap();
    }

    /// Logs one event per target and level through `directives` and returns what got through
    fn logged_through(directives: &str) -> String {
        let lines = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::<u8>::new()));
        let writer = {
            let lines = lines.clone();
            move || SharedWriter(lines.clone())
        };

        let subscriber = Registry::default().with(
            tracing_subscriber::fmt::layer()
                .with_ansi(false)
                .with_writer(writer)
                .with_filter(console_filter(directives)),
        );

        tracing::subscriber::with_default(subscriber, || {
            tracing::debug!(target: "reliquary_archiver::session", "app debug");
            tracing::info!(target: "reliquary_archiver::session", "app info");
            tracing::info!(target: "reliquary::network", "library info");
            tracing::warn!(target: "reliquary::network::kcp", "library warning");
            tracing::info!(target: "pktmon::realtime", "pktmon info");
            tracing::warn!(target: "pktmon::realtime", "pktmon warning");
            tracing::warn!(target: "hyper", "unrelated warning");
        });

        String::from_utf8(lines.lock().unwrap().clone()).unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn default_directives_show_library_warnings_only() {
        let output = logged_through(DEFAULT_DIRECTIVES);
        assert!(output.contains("app info"));
        assert!(output.contains("library warning"));
        assert!(output.contains("pktmon warning"));
        assert!(!output.contains("app debug"));
        assert!(!output.contains("library info"));
        assert!(!output.contains("pktmon info"));
        assert!(!output.contains("unrelated warning"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn debug_directives_show_library_info() {
        let output = logged_through(DEBUG_DIRECTIVES);
        assert!(output.contains("app debug"));
        assert!(output.contains("library info"));
        assert!(output.contains("pktmon info"));
        assert!(!output.contains("unrelated warning"));
    }

    struct SharedWriter(Arc<Mutex<Vec<u8>>>);

    impl io::Write for SharedWriter {
        fn write(&mut self, buf: &[u8]) -> io::Result<usize> {
            self.0.lock().unwrap().extend_from_slice(buf);
            Ok(buf.len())
        }

        fn flush(&mut self) -> io::Result<()> {
            Ok(())
        }
    }
}
```

```rs
#![cfg_attr(all(windows, feature = "gui"), windows_subsystem = "windows")]

use std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe;
use std::path::PathBuf;

use clap::Parser;
use futures::FutureExt;
use tracing::{debug, error, info};

#[cfg(feature = "stream")]
mod websocket;

#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
mod rgui;

#[cfg(windows)]
mod update;

#[cfg(all(not(feature = "pcap"), feature = "pktmon"))]
mod elevation;

mod accounts;
mod capture;
mod cli;
mod logging;
mod merge;
mod session;
mod stall;

#[derive(Parser, Debug, Clone)]
struct Args {
    #[arg()]
    /// Path to output .json file to, per default: archive_output-%Y-%m-%dT%H-%M-%S.json
    output: Option<PathBuf>,

    /// Read packets from .pcap file instead of capturing live packets
    #[cfg(feature = "pcap")]
    #[arg(long)]
    pcap: Option<PathBuf>,

    /// Read packets from .etl file instead of capturing live packets
    #[cfg(feature = "pktmon")]
    #[arg(long)]
    etl: Option<PathBuf>,

    /// How long to wait in seconds until timeout is triggered for live captures
    #[arg(long, default_value_t = 120)]
    timeout: u64,

    /// Host a websocket server to stream relic/lc updates in real-time.
    /// This also disables the timeout
    #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
    #[arg(short, long)]
    stream: bool,

    /// Port to listen on for the websocket server, defaults to 23313
    #[cfg(feature = "stream")]
    #[arg(short = 'p', long, default_value_t = 23313)]
    websocket_port: u16,

    /// How verbose the output should be, can be set up to 3 times. Has no effect if RUST_LOG is set
    #[arg(short, long, action = clap::ArgAction::Count)]
    verbose: u8,

    /// Path to output log to
    #[arg(short, long)]
    log_path: Option<PathBuf>,

    /// Don't check for updates, only applicable on Windows
    #[arg(long)]
    no_update: bool,

    /// Update without asking for confirmation, only applicable on Windows
    #[arg(long)]
    always_update: bool,

    /// Github Auth token to use when checking for updates, only applicable on Windows
    #[arg(long)]
    auth_token: Option<String>,

    /// Don't wait for enter to be pressed after capturing
    #[arg(short, long)]
    exit_after_capture: bool,
    /// Run in headless mode (no GUI), only applicable when GUI feature is enabled
    #[cfg(feature = "gui")]
    #[arg(long, short = 'H', visible_alias = "cli", visible_alias = "nogui")]
    headless: bool,

    /// Detach from the parent terminal (run in background), only applicable on Windows
    #[cfg(all(windows, feature = "gui"))]
    #[arg(long, short = 'd')]
    detach: bool,
}

#[cfg(not(any(feature = "pktmon", feature = "pcap")))]
compile_error!("Either \"pktmon\" (windows exclusive) or \"pcap\" must be enabled");

#[cfg(all(not(windows), feature = "gui"))]
compile_error!("GUI is only available on windows");

#[cfg(all(not(windows), feature = "pktmon"))]
compile_error!("The \"pktmon\" capture backend is only available on windows");

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() {
    color_eyre::install().unwrap();

    let old_hook = std::panic::take_hook();
    std::panic::set_hook(Box::new(move |panic_info| {
        let backtrace = std::backtrace::Backtrace::force_capture();
        old_hook(panic_info);
        error!("Backtrace: {:#?}", backtrace);
    }));

    // Attach to parent console on Windows GUI builds
    // This is needed for --help output and headless mode to be visible
    // AttachConsole fails if no parent console exists (e.g. double-clicked from Explorer)
    #[cfg(all(windows, feature = "gui"))]
    let has_console =
        unsafe { windows::Win32::System::Console::AttachConsole(windows::Win32::System::Console::ATTACH_PARENT_PROCESS).is_ok() };

    let args = Args::parse();

    // Detach from parent terminal if requested
    #[cfg(all(windows, feature = "gui"))]
    if args.detach {
        unsafe { windows::Win32::System::Console::FreeConsole().ok() };
    }

    // Allocate a console for headless mode if AttachConsole didn't work
    #[cfg(all(windows, feature = "gui"))]
    if args.headless && !has_console && !args.detach {
        unsafe { windows::Win32::System::Console::AllocConsole().ok() };
    }

    logging::tracing_init(args.verbose, args.log_path.as_deref());

    debug!(?args);

    // AssertUnwindSafe is justified as all we do is write a crash log before ending the program and therefore there is no risk posed by potential broken invariants
    if let Err(payload) = AssertUnwindSafe(run(args)).catch_unwind().await {
        error!("the application panicked, this is a bug, please report it on GitHub or Discord");

        logging::write_crashlog(payload.as_ref());

        info!("press enter to close");
        std::io::stdin().read_line(&mut String::new()).unwrap();
    }
}

async fn run(args: Args) {
    // Only self update on Windows, since that's the only platform we ship releases for
    // In GUI mode, the update check is handled by the GUI after it launches
    #[cfg(windows)]
    {
        #[cfg(feature = "gui")]
        let gui_mode = !args.headless;
        #[cfg(not(feature = "gui"))]
        let gui_mode = false;

        if !gui_mode && !args.no_update && !std::env::var("NO_SELF_UPDATE").is_ok_and(|v| v == "1") {
            if let Err(e) = update::update_interactive(args.auth_token.as_deref(), args.always_update) {
                error!("Failed to update: {}", e);
            }
        }
    }

    #[cfg(all(not(feature = "pcap"), feature = "pktmon"))]
    if !elevation::is_admin() {
        elevation::prompt_and_escalate();
        return;
    }

    #[cfg(feature = "gui")]
    if !args.headless {
        rgui::run().unwrap();

        // Check if we need to spawn the updated version after GUI exits
        if update::should_spawn_after_exit() {
            if let Err(e) = update::spawn_updated_version() {
                error!("Failed to spawn updated version: {}", e);
            }
        }
        return;
    }

    cli::run(args).await;
}
```

`src/merge.rs`:

```rs
//! Merges what several capture sources decoded from the same traffic.
//!
//! The same packet is often seen by more than one source: pcap on several interfaces, or pktmon
//! reporting it once for every component it passes through. Each source decodes its copies with
//! its own sniffer, so a source that misses or mangles a packet only breaks itself, and
//! [`SourceMerger`] passes each message on the first time any source produces it.
//!
//! Messages are matched by content. A message the game really sends twice is still passed on
//! twice: every source sees both, and a copy only counts as new once its source has produced it
//! more often than it has been passed on.

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::hash::{DefaultHasher, Hash, Hasher};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use reliquary::network::command::GameCommand;

/// How long a message is remembered after its latest copy. Sources see the same packet within
/// milliseconds of each other, this only has to outlast a source that's briefly behind.
const MESSAGE_TTL: Duration = Duration::from_secs(60);

/// How often forgotten messages are cleaned up
const PRUNE_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_secs(10);

/// Identifies a message across sources
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub enum MessageKey {
    HandshakeEstablished { conv_id: u32 },
    Disconnected,
    Command { conv_id: u32, hash: u64 },
}

impl MessageKey {
    pub fn command(conv_id: u32, command: &GameCommand) -> Self {
        let mut hasher = DefaultHasher::new();
        command.command_id.hash(&mut hasher);
        command.proto_header.hash(&mut hasher);
        command.proto_data.hash(&mut hasher);

        MessageKey::Command {
            conv_id,
            hash: hasher.finish(),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct Seen {
    /// How often this message has been passed on
    delivered: u32,
    /// How often each source has produced it
    per_source: HashMap<u64, u32>,
    last_seen: Instant,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct SourceMerger {
    seen: HashMap<MessageKey, Seen>,
    last_prune: Option<Instant>,
}

impl Default for SourceMerger {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl SourceMerger {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            seen: HashMap::new(),
            last_prune: None,
        }
    }

    /// Whether `source` producing `key` is news, rather than a copy of something another source
    /// already produced
    pub fn accept(&mut self, source: u64, key: MessageKey, now: Instant) -> bool {
        self.prune(now);

        let seen = self.seen.entry(key).or_insert_with(|| Seen {
            delivered: 0,
            per_source: HashMap::new(),
            last_seen: now,
        });
        seen.last_seen = now;

        let produced = seen.per_source.entry(source).or_default();
        *produced += 1;

        if *produced > seen.delivered {
            seen.delivered = *produced;
            true
        } else {
            false
        }
    }

    fn prune(&mut self, now: Instant) {
        if self
            .last_prune
            .is_some_and(|last| now.saturating_duration_since(last) < PRUNE_INTERVAL)
        {
            return;
        }

        self.last_prune = Some(now);
        self.seen
            .retain(|_, seen| now.saturating_duration_since(seen.last_seen) < MESSAGE_TTL);
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const A: u64 = 1;
    const B: u64 = 2;
    const C: u64 = 3;

    fn command(n: u64) -> MessageKey {
        MessageKey::Command { conv_id: 7, hash: n }
    }

    #[test]
    fn first_copy_wins() {
        let now = Instant::now();
        let mut merger = SourceMerger::new();

        assert!(merger.accept(A, command(1), now));
        assert!(!merger.accept(B, command(1), now));
        assert!(!merger.accept(C, command(1), now));
    }

    #[test]
    fn repeated_messages_are_delivered_each_time() {
        let now = Instant::now();
        let mut merger = SourceMerger::new();

        // The game sends the same message twice, both sources see both
        assert!(merger.accept(A, command(1), now));
        assert!(!merger.accept(B, command(1), now));
        assert!(merger.accept(A, command(1), now));
        assert!(!merger.accept(B, command(1), now));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_lagging_source_takes_over_where_the_other_stopped() {
        let now = Instant::now();
        let mut merger = SourceMerger::new();

        // A delivers 1 and 2, then misses a packet and stops decoding
        assert!(merger.accept(A, command(1), now));
        assert!(merger.accept(A, command(2), now));

        // B was behind, catches up on what A delivered, then carries on alone
        assert!(!merger.accept(B, command(1), now));
        assert!(!merger.accept(B, command(2), now));
        assert!(merger.accept(B, command(3), now));
        assert!(merger.accept(B, command(4), now));
    }

    #[test]
    fn copies_from_one_source_are_not_lost_to_another_sources_repeats() {
        let now = Instant::now();
        let mut merger = SourceMerger::new();

        // A sees the message twice before B sees it at all
        assert!(merger.accept(A, command(1), now));
        assert!(merger.accept(A, command(1), now));
        assert!(!merger.accept(B, command(1), now));
        assert!(!merger.accept(B, command(1), now));
        // Only a third copy from B is news
        assert!(merger.accept(B, command(1), now));
    }

    #[test]
    fn keys_are_independent() {
        let now = Instant::now();
        let mut merger = SourceMerger::new();

        assert!(merger.accept(A, MessageKey::HandshakeEstablished { conv_id: 7 }, now));
        assert!(merger.accept(A, MessageKey::HandshakeEstablished { conv_id: 8 }, now));
        assert!(merger.accept(A, command(1), now));
        assert!(merger.accept(A, MessageKey::Command { conv_id: 8, hash: 1 }, now));
    }

    #[test]
    fn messages_are_forgotten_after_a_while() {
        let start = Instant::now();
        let mut merger = SourceMerger::new();

        assert!(merger.accept(A, MessageKey::Disconnected, start));
        assert!(!merger.accept(B, MessageKey::Disconnected, start));

        // A later disconnect, long after the first one was forgotten
        let later = start + MESSAGE_TTL + PRUNE_INTERVAL;
        assert!(merger.accept(B, MessageKey::Disconnected, later));
        assert!(!merger.accept(A, MessageKey::Disconnected, later));
        assert!(merger.seen.len() == 1);
    }
}
```
