`src/rgui/worker.rs`:

```rs
use std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use futures::channel::mpsc;
use futures::lock::Mutex;
use futures::sink::SinkExt;
use futures::{FutureExt, Stream, StreamExt, stream};
use reliquary_archiver::export::fribbels::OptimizerEvent;
use tokio::sync::broadcast::error::RecvError;
use tracing::{error, info, instrument, warn};

use crate::accounts::{AccountEvent, MultiAccountManager};
use crate::capture::{self, SourceSpec};
use crate::session::{CaptureSession, SessionEvent};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum WorkerEvent {
    /// A packet arrived from the network
    PacketReceived,
    /// What the capture session made of a packet
    Session(SessionEvent),
    /// An account logged in, for the first time or again on a new connection
    AccountLoggedIn {
        uid: u32,
    },
    ExportEvent {
        uid: u32,
        event: OptimizerEvent,
    },
}

/// Creates a new [`Stream`] that produces the items sent from a [`Future`]
/// to the [`mpsc::Sender`] provided to the closure.
///
/// This is a more ergonomic [`stream::unfold`], which allows you to go
/// from the "world of futures" to the "world of streams" by simply looping
/// and publishing to an async channel from inside a [`Future`].
pub fn stream_channel<T>(size: usize, f: impl AsyncFnOnce(mpsc::Sender<T>)) -> impl Stream<Item = T> {
    let (sender, receiver) = mpsc::channel(size);

    let runner = stream::once(f(sender)).filter_map(|_| async { None });

    stream::select(receiver, runner)
}

#[instrument(skip_all)]
pub fn archiver_worker(manager: Arc<Mutex<MultiAccountManager>>) -> impl Stream<Item = WorkerEvent> {
    stream_channel(100, |mut output: mpsc::Sender<WorkerEvent>| async move {
        tokio::spawn({
            let capture = live_capture(manager.clone(), output.clone());

            async move {
                // Without this a panic would end the capture silently while the window stays open
                if AssertUnwindSafe(capture).catch_unwind().await.is_err() {
                    error!("the packet capture panicked and has stopped, please restart the application");
                }
            }
        });

        // Subscribe to manager's unified event channel
        let mut manager_rx = manager.lock().await.subscribe();

        // Forward events from manager's unified event channel
        loop {
            let event = match manager_rx.recv().await {
                Ok(event) => event,
                Err(RecvError::Lagged(skipped)) => {
                    warn!(skipped, "worker fell behind on account events, some were dropped");
                    continue;
                }
                Err(RecvError::Closed) => break,
            };

            let event = match event {
                AccountEvent::Discovered { uid } | AccountEvent::Reconnected { uid } => WorkerEvent::AccountLoggedIn { uid },
                AccountEvent::ExporterEvent { uid, event } => WorkerEvent::ExportEvent { uid, event },
            };

            output.send(event).await.ok();
        }
    })
}

#[instrument(skip_all)]
async fn live_capture(manager: Arc<Mutex<MultiAccountManager>>, mut output: mpsc::Sender<WorkerEvent>) {
    // The GUI keeps capturing for as long as it runs, a single bad packet shouldn't cost it a network interface
    let mut session = CaptureSession::new(manager, false);

    // Outer loop to restart capture when it exits
    loop {
        let mut packets = match capture::open_source(&SourceSpec::Live) {
            Ok(packets) => packets,
            Err(e) => {
                warn!(error = %e, "Failed to start packet capture, retrying in 1 second...");
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(1)).await;
                continue;
            }
        };

        info!("instructions: go to main menu screen and go to the \"Click to Start\" screen");

        while let Some(packet) = packets.next().await {
            let packet = match packet {
                Ok(packet) => packet,
                Err(e) => {
                    warn!(%e);
                    continue;
                }
            };

            output.send(WorkerEvent::PacketReceived).await.ok();

            for event in session.handle_packet(packet).await {
                output.send(WorkerEvent::Session(event)).await.ok();
            }
        }

        warn!("live capture stream ended unexpectedly");
        info!("capture ended, restarting...");
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(10)).await;
    }
}

```

`src/session.rs`:

```rs
//! Turns captured packets into exporter state.
//!
//! A [`CaptureSession`] is the single place where packets are decoded and routed to the
//! per-account exporters. It reports what happened as [`SessionEvent`]s and leaves the policy
//! (when to stop, what to show the user) to whoever drives it: the CLI or the GUI worker.

use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Instant;

use futures::lock::Mutex;
use reliquary::network::command::command_id::{PlayerGetTokenScRsp, PlayerLoginFinishScRsp, PlayerLoginScRsp};
use reliquary::network::command::proto::PlayerGetTokenScRsp::PlayerGetTokenScRsp as PlayerGetTokenScRspProto;
use reliquary::network::command::{GameCommand, GameCommandError};
use reliquary::network::{ConnectionPacket, ConnectionPacketError, GamePacket, GameSniffer, NetworkError};
use reliquary_archiver::export::Exporter;
use reliquary_archiver::export::fribbels::{Export, OptimizerExporter};
use tracing::{info, trace, warn};

use crate::accounts::MultiAccountManager;
use crate::capture::Packet;
use crate::merge::{MessageKey, SourceMerger};
use crate::stall::{STALL_THRESHOLD, StallChange, StallDetector};

/// Something that happened while processing a packet
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SessionEvent {
    ConnectionEstablished,
    Disconnected,
    /// A packet was decoded into this many game packets
    PacketsDecoded(usize),
    /// An account logged in. Reported on every login, also for accounts that have been seen before
    AccountSeen {
        uid: u32,
    },
    /// The game finished sending its login data
    LoginFinished,
    /// The traffic can't be decrypted, which means the game version is not supported
    DecryptionKeyMissing,
    /// A packet or command could not be decoded, capturing can continue
    DecodeError,
    /// Game traffic keeps arriving but nothing decodes from it, so the capture missed data and
    /// the rest of this connection can't be read. Logging in again starts a fresh connection.
    Stalled,
    /// Commands are being decoded again after [`SessionEvent::Stalled`]
    Recovered,
}

/// Decodes packets into commands for the exporters.
///
/// Every capture source gets its own sniffer. Sources often see the same traffic (several network
/// interfaces, or pktmon reporting a packet once per component it passes), and a source that
/// misses or mangles a packet would otherwise break decoding for all of them. What the sniffers
/// decode goes through a [`SourceMerger`], so each message reaches the exporters once, from
/// whichever source produced it first.
pub struct CaptureSession {
    sniffers: HashMap<u64, GameSniffer>,
    merger: SourceMerger,
    manager: Arc<Mutex<MultiAccountManager>>,
    /// Sources that sent something that isn't game traffic and are ignored from then on
    poisoned_sources: Option<HashSet<u64>>,
    latest_uid: Option<u32>,
    stall: StallDetector,
}

impl CaptureSession {
    /// `poison_sources` makes the session ignore a source for good once it delivers a packet
    /// that can't be parsed, which is what short-lived captures over all network interfaces want.
    pub fn new(manager: Arc<Mutex<MultiAccountManager>>, poison_sources: bool) -> Self {
        Self {
            sniffers: HashMap::new(),
            merger: SourceMerger::new(),
            manager,
            poisoned_sources: poison_sources.then(HashSet::new),
            latest_uid: None,
            stall: StallDetector::default(),
        }
    }

    pub async fn handle_packet(&mut self, packet: Packet) -> Vec<SessionEvent> {
        let mut events = self.decode_packet(packet).await;

        // Anything that got this far was game traffic, whether or not it decoded
        if !events.is_empty() {
            let change = self.stall.traffic(Instant::now());
            push_stall_change(&mut events, change);
        }

        events
    }

    /// Decodes the packet and routes its commands. Returns no events for packets that aren't game traffic.
    async fn decode_packet(&mut self, packet: Packet) -> Vec<SessionEvent> {
        let mut events = Vec::new();
        let source = packet.source_id;

        if self.poisoned_sources.as_ref().is_some_and(|sources| sources.contains(&source)) {
            // We already know that this source is poisoned, so we can skip it
            return events;
        }

        let sniffer = self.sniffers.entry(source).or_default();
        let packets = match sniffer.receive_packet(packet.data) {
            Ok(packets) => packets,
            Err(NetworkError::ConnectionPacket(ConnectionPacketError::IncorrectPorts)) => {
                // Not game traffic, which is expected from sources that don't filter by port
                trace!("ignoring packet with unrelated ports");
                return events;
            }
            Err(e) => {
                warn!(source, %e);
                events.push(self.network_error_event(e, source));
                return events;
            }
        };

        let now = Instant::now();
        let mut delivered = 0;
        let mut delivered_events = Vec::new();

        for packet in packets {
            let key = match &packet {
                GamePacket::Connection(ConnectionPacket::HandshakeEstablished { conv_id }) => {
                    Some(MessageKey::HandshakeEstablished { conv_id: *conv_id })
                }
                GamePacket::Connection(ConnectionPacket::Disconnected) => Some(MessageKey::Disconnected),
                GamePacket::Commands {
                    conv_id,
                    result: Ok(command),
                } => Some(MessageKey::command(*conv_id, command)),
                // Errors are this source's own, report them as they come
                GamePacket::Commands { result: Err(_), .. } | GamePacket::Connection(_) => None,
            };

            if let Some(key) = key {
                if !self.merger.accept(source, key, now) {
                    // Another source got here first
                    continue;
                }
                delivered += 1;
            }

            self.handle_game_packet(packet, &mut delivered_events).await;
        }

        events.push(SessionEvent::PacketsDecoded(delivered));
        events.append(&mut delivered_events);
        events
    }

    async fn handle_game_packet(&mut self, packet: GamePacket, events: &mut Vec<SessionEvent>) {
        match packet {
            GamePacket::Connection(ConnectionPacket::HandshakeEstablished { conv_id }) => {
                info!(conv_id, "detected connection established");

                if cfg!(all(feature = "pcap", windows)) {
                    info!(
                        "If the program gets stuck at this point for longer than 10 seconds, please try the pktmon release from https://github.com/IceDynamix/reliquary-archiver/releases/latest"
                    );
                }

                events.push(SessionEvent::ConnectionEstablished);

                let change = self.stall.connection_established(Instant::now());
                push_stall_change(events, change);
            }
            GamePacket::Connection(ConnectionPacket::Disconnected) => {
                info!("detected connection disconnected");
                events.push(SessionEvent::Disconnected);

                let change = self.stall.disconnected();
                push_stall_change(events, change);
            }
            GamePacket::Connection(_) => {}
            GamePacket::Commands {
                conv_id,
                result: Ok(command),
            } => {
                // Any source decoding counts as progress, and only the first copy gets this far
                let change = self.stall.command_decoded(Instant::now());
                push_stall_change(events, change);

                self.handle_command(conv_id, command, events).await;
            }
            GamePacket::Commands { conv_id, result: Err(e) } => {
                warn!(conv_id, %e);
                events.push(command_error_event(&e));
            }
        }
    }

    async fn handle_command(&mut self, conv_id: u32, command: GameCommand, events: &mut Vec<SessionEvent>) {
        if command.command_id == PlayerLoginScRsp {
            info!(conv_id, "detected login start");
        }

        if command.command_id == PlayerLoginFinishScRsp {
            events.push(SessionEvent::LoginFinished);
        }

        // Route command to the correct exporter
        let exporter = self.manager.lock().await.get_or_create_exporter(conv_id);

        // Check if this is a UID discovery packet and register it if so
        if command.command_id == PlayerGetTokenScRsp
            && let Ok(token_rsp) = command.parse_proto::<PlayerGetTokenScRspProto>()
        {
            let uid = token_rsp.uid;
            self.manager.lock().await.register_uid(conv_id, uid);
            self.latest_uid = Some(uid);
            events.push(SessionEvent::AccountSeen { uid });
        }

        exporter.lock().await.read_command(command);
    }

    fn network_error_event(&mut self, error: NetworkError, source_id: u64) -> SessionEvent {
        match error {
            NetworkError::ConnectionPacket(e) => {
                if let ConnectionPacketError::TransportLayerNotPresent = e {
                    warn!("This error tends to happen when using VPNs or similar programs. Please disable them and try again.");
                }

                // Connection errors are not fatal as all network interfaces are funneled through the same stream
                // Just mark this source as poisoned and continue listening on other sources
                if let Some(sources) = self.poisoned_sources.as_mut() {
                    sources.insert(source_id);
                    self.sniffers.remove(&source_id);
                }

                SessionEvent::DecodeError
            }
            // Each source decodes on its own, so a KCP error only affects the source it came from
            // and the others carry on
            NetworkError::Kcp(_) => SessionEvent::DecodeError,
            NetworkError::GameCommand(e) => command_error_event(&e),
        }
    }

    /// The exporter of the account that logged in most recently
    pub async fn latest_exporter(&self) -> Option<Arc<Mutex<OptimizerExporter>>> {
        self.manager.lock().await.get_account_exporter(self.latest_uid?)
    }

    /// Whether everything needed for an export of the latest account has been captured
    pub async fn is_initialized(&self) -> bool {
        match self.latest_exporter().await {
            Some(exporter) => exporter.lock().await.is_initialized(),
            None => false,
        }
    }

    /// Export whatever has been captured for the latest account
    pub async fn export(&self) -> Option<Export> {
        self.latest_exporter().await?.lock().await.export()
    }
}

fn push_stall_change(events: &mut Vec<SessionEvent>, change: Option<StallChange>) {
    match change {
        Some(StallChange::Stalled) => {
            warn!(
                "game traffic is still arriving, but nothing could be decoded from it for {} seconds. The capture missed some data, \
                 so the rest of this login can't be read. Log out to the title screen and log back in to capture again.",
                STALL_THRESHOLD.as_secs()
            );
            events.push(SessionEvent::Stalled);
        }
        Some(StallChange::Recovered) => {
            info!("capture is decoding game data again");
            events.push(SessionEvent::Recovered);
        }
        None => {}
    }
}

fn command_error_event(error: &GameCommandError) -> SessionEvent {
    match error {
        // version is not supported, there's no point in capturing
        GameCommandError::DecryptionKeyMissing => SessionEvent::DecryptionKeyMissing,
        // HeaderTooShort/CommandTooShort are structural parse errors.
        // VersionMismatch means the client packet was likely misordered from the server packet,
        // it will be reprocessed after we receive the new session key
        _ => SessionEvent::DecodeError,
    }
}

/// Reads a recorded capture from start to end and exports the account found in it
#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
pub async fn export_from_file(spec: crate::capture::SourceSpec) -> Option<Export> {
    use futures::StreamExt;

    let mut packets = match crate::capture::open_source(&spec) {
        Ok(packets) => packets,
        Err(e) => {
            tracing::error!(%e, "could not read capture file");
            return None;
        }
    };

    let mut session = CaptureSession::new(Arc::new(Mutex::new(MultiAccountManager::new())), false);

    while let Some(packet) = packets.next().await {
        match packet {
            Ok(packet) => {
                session.handle_packet(packet).await;
            }
            Err(e) => warn!(%e),
        }

        if session.is_initialized().await {
            info!("finished capturing");
            break;
        }
    }

    session.export().await
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    /// An Ethernet/IPv4/UDP frame from the game server carrying `payload`
    fn server_frame(payload: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {
        let udp_len = 8 + payload.len() as u16;
        let ip_len = 20 + udp_len;

        let mut frame = Vec::new();
        frame.extend_from_slice(&[0; 12]); // MAC addresses
        frame.extend_from_slice(&0x0800u16.to_be_bytes()); // IPv4
        frame.extend_from_slice(&[0x45, 0]);
        frame.extend_from_slice(&ip_len.to_be_bytes());
        frame.extend_from_slice(&[0, 0, 0, 0, 64, 17, 0, 0]); // id, flags, ttl, UDP, checksum
        frame.extend_from_slice(&[10, 0, 0, 1, 10, 0, 0, 2]); // addresses
        frame.extend_from_slice(&23301u16.to_be_bytes());
        frame.extend_from_slice(&50000u16.to_be_bytes());
        frame.extend_from_slice(&udp_len.to_be_bytes());
        frame.extend_from_slice(&[0, 0]); // checksum
        frame.extend_from_slice(payload);
        frame
    }

    fn handshake_established(conv_id: u32) -> Vec<u8> {
        let mut payload = [0u8; 20];
        payload[..4].copy_from_slice(&0x145u32.to_be_bytes());
        payload[4..8].copy_from_slice(&conv_id.to_be_bytes());
        server_frame(&payload)
    }

    fn disconnected() -> Vec<u8> {
        let mut payload = [0u8; 20];
        payload[..4].copy_from_slice(&404u32.to_be_bytes());
        server_frame(&payload)
    }

    fn session() -> CaptureSession {
        CaptureSession::new(Arc::new(Mutex::new(MultiAccountManager::new())), false)
    }

    /// Feeds `data` to the session once per source and collects every event
    async fn from_each_source(session: &mut CaptureSession, sources: &[u64], data: &[u8]) -> Vec<SessionEvent> {
        let mut events = Vec::new();
        for &source_id in sources {
            let packet = Packet {
                source_id,
                data: data.to_vec(),
            };
            events.extend(session.handle_packet(packet).await);
        }
        events
    }

    fn count(events: &[SessionEvent], event: SessionEvent) -> usize {
        events.iter().filter(|e| **e == event).count()
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn the_same_handshake_from_several_sources_is_reported_once() {
        let mut session = session();

        let events = from_each_source(&mut session, &[1, 2, 3], &handshake_established(7)).await;
        assert_eq!(count(&events, SessionEvent::ConnectionEstablished), 1);
        assert_eq!(count(&events, SessionEvent::PacketsDecoded(1)), 1);
        assert_eq!(count(&events, SessionEvent::PacketsDecoded(0)), 2);

        // Each source still decodes on its own
        assert_eq!(session.sniffers.len(), 3);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn a_new_connection_is_reported_again() {
        let mut session = session();

        from_each_source(&mut session, &[1, 2], &handshake_established(7)).await;
        let events = from_each_source(&mut session, &[1, 2], &handshake_established(8)).await;
        assert_eq!(count(&events, SessionEvent::ConnectionEstablished), 1);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn the_same_disconnect_from_several_sources_is_reported_once() {
        let mut session = session();

        let events = from_each_source(&mut session, &[1, 2, 3], &disconnected()).await;
        assert_eq!(count(&events, SessionEvent::Disconnected), 1);
    }
}
```

`src/stall.rs`:

```rs
//! Notices when a capture stops making progress.
//!
//! The game's traffic is KCP, which delivers commands strictly in order. The sniffer only watches,
//! so it can't ask for a retransmit: once the capture misses a segment (pktmon dropping packets
//! under load, for example), every later command waits on it forever. Packets keep arriving, but
//! nothing decodes, and the capture looks connected while it collects nothing.
//!
//! A healthy connection decodes commands regularly, even when the player is idle, because the
//! client and server exchange heartbeats. So game traffic that keeps arriving without a single
//! decoded command for a while means the capture has lost data.

use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// How long game traffic may arrive without any decoded command before the capture counts as stalled.
/// An idle connection can go about two minutes between heartbeats, so this leaves headroom above
/// that: a false alarm is worse than a late one.
pub const STALL_THRESHOLD: Duration = Duration::from_secs(3 * 60);

/// A change in whether the capture is making progress
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum StallChange {
    /// Traffic kept arriving, but nothing could be decoded from it for [`STALL_THRESHOLD`]
    Stalled,
    /// Commands are being decoded again after a stall
    Recovered,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct StallDetector {
    threshold: Duration,
    /// When a command was last decoded, or the connection was established.
    /// `None` until then, since traffic from a connection that was already running when the
    /// capture started never decodes, and that's not a stall.
    last_progress: Option<Instant>,
    stalled: bool,
}

impl Default for StallDetector {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new(STALL_THRESHOLD)
    }
}

impl StallDetector {
    pub fn new(threshold: Duration) -> Self {
        Self {
            threshold,
            last_progress: None,
            stalled: false,
        }
    }

    /// A connection was established, which starts the clock
    pub fn connection_established(&mut self, now: Instant) -> Option<StallChange> {
        self.progress(now)
    }

    /// A command was decoded
    pub fn command_decoded(&mut self, now: Instant) -> Option<StallChange> {
        self.progress(now)
    }

    /// The connection ended, there's nothing left to stall
    pub fn disconnected(&mut self) -> Option<StallChange> {
        self.last_progress = None;
        std::mem::take(&mut self.stalled).then_some(StallChange::Recovered)
    }

    /// Game traffic arrived. Reports [`StallChange::Stalled`] once it has gone on for too long
    /// without progress.
    pub fn traffic(&mut self, now: Instant) -> Option<StallChange> {
        let last_progress = self.last_progress?;

        if !self.stalled && now.saturating_duration_since(last_progress) >= self.threshold {
            self.stalled = true;
            return Some(StallChange::Stalled);
        }

        None
    }

    fn progress(&mut self, now: Instant) -> Option<StallChange> {
        self.last_progress = Some(now);
        std::mem::take(&mut self.stalled).then_some(StallChange::Recovered)
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const THRESHOLD: Duration = Duration::from_secs(30);

    fn secs(start: Instant, s: u64) -> Instant {
        start + Duration::from_secs(s)
    }

    #[test]
    fn traffic_before_any_connection_never_stalls() {
        let start = Instant::now();
        let mut detector = StallDetector::new(THRESHOLD);

        assert_eq!(detector.traffic(start), None);
        assert_eq!(detector.traffic(secs(start, 600)), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn steady_commands_never_stall() {
        let start = Instant::now();
        let mut detector = StallDetector::new(THRESHOLD);
        detector.connection_established(start);

        for s in (5..300).step_by(5) {
            assert_eq!(detector.traffic(secs(start, s)), None);
            assert_eq!(detector.command_decoded(secs(start, s)), None);
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn traffic_without_commands_stalls_once() {
        let start = Instant::now();
        let mut detector = StallDetector::new(THRESHOLD);
        detector.connection_established(start);
        detector.command_decoded(secs(start, 1));

        assert_eq!(detector.traffic(secs(start, 30)), None);
        assert_eq!(detector.traffic(secs(start, 31)), Some(StallChange::Stalled));
        assert_eq!(detector.traffic(secs(start, 60)), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn silence_is_not_a_stall() {
        let start = Instant::now();
        let mut detector = StallDetector::new(THRESHOLD);
        detector.connection_established(start);

        // Nothing arrived in between, so there's no traffic that failed to decode
        assert_eq!(detector.command_decoded(secs(start, 120)), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn handshake_without_commands_stalls() {
        let start = Instant::now();
        let mut detector = StallDetector::new(THRESHOLD);
        detector.connection_established(start);

        assert_eq!(detector.traffic(secs(start, 30)), Some(StallChange::Stalled));
    }

    #[test]
    fn new_connection_recovers() {
        let start = Instant::now();
        let mut detector = StallDetector::new(THRESHOLD);
        detector.connection_established(start);
        detector.traffic(secs(start, 30));

        assert_eq!(detector.connection_established(secs(start, 40)), Some(StallChange::Recovered));
        assert_eq!(detector.traffic(secs(start, 50)), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn decoded_command_recovers() {
        let start = Instant::now();
        let mut detector = StallDetector::new(THRESHOLD);
        detector.connection_established(start);
        detector.traffic(secs(start, 30));

        assert_eq!(detector.command_decoded(secs(start, 35)), Some(StallChange::Recovered));
    }

    #[test]
    fn disconnect_recovers_and_disarms() {
        let start = Instant::now();
        let mut detector = StallDetector::new(THRESHOLD);
        detector.connection_established(start);
        detector.traffic(secs(start, 30));

        assert_eq!(detector.disconnected(), Some(StallChange::Recovered));
        assert_eq!(detector.traffic(secs(start, 600)), None);
    }
}
```

`src/update.rs`:

```rs
//! Self-update functionality for Windows builds

use std::env;
use std::process::Command;
#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};

use self_update::cargo_crate_version;
use tracing::info;

/// Flag to indicate that the updated version should be spawned after GUI exits
#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
static SPAWN_UPDATED_VERSION: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);

/// Set the flag to spawn updated version after GUI exits
#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
pub fn request_spawn_after_exit() {
    SPAWN_UPDATED_VERSION.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
}

/// Check if we should spawn the updated version
#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
pub fn should_spawn_after_exit() -> bool {
    SPAWN_UPDATED_VERSION.load(Ordering::SeqCst)
}

const REPO_OWNER: &str = "IceDynamix";
const REPO_NAME: &str = "reliquary-archiver";
const TARGET: &str = "x64";

fn identifier() -> Option<&'static str> {
    if cfg!(feature = "pcap") {
        Some("pcap")
    } else if cfg!(feature = "pktmon") {
        Some("pktmon")
    } else {
        None
    }
}

fn configure_builder() -> self_update::backends::github::UpdateBuilder {
    let mut builder = self_update::backends::github::Update::configure();
    builder
        .repo_owner(REPO_OWNER)
        .repo_name(REPO_NAME)
        .bin_name(REPO_NAME)
        .target(TARGET)
        .current_version(cargo_crate_version!());
    if let Some(id) = identifier() {
        builder.identifier(id);
    }
    builder
}

/// Interactive update for CLI mode (shows download progress, prompts user)
/// Spawns updated version after GUI exits if update is successful
pub fn update_interactive(auth_token: Option<&str>, no_confirm: bool) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> {
    info!("checking for updates");

    let mut update_builder = configure_builder();
    update_builder
        .show_download_progress(true)
        .show_output(false)
        .no_confirm(no_confirm);

    if let Some(token) = auth_token {
        update_builder.auth_token(token);
    }

    let status = update_builder.build()?.update()?;

    if status.updated() {
        info!("updated to {}", status.version());
        spawn_updated_version()?;
    } else {
        info!("already up-to-date");
    }

    Ok(())
}

/// Perform the update (should be called after user confirms)
/// Returns true if the update was successful and the app should restart
#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
pub fn update_noninteractive() -> Result<bool, Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> {
    let mut update_builder = configure_builder();
    update_builder.show_download_progress(false).show_output(false).no_confirm(true);

    let status = update_builder.build()?.update()?;

    if status.updated() {
        info!("updated to {}", status.version());
        return Ok(true);
    }

    Ok(false)
}

/// Information about an available update
#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct UpdateInfo {
    pub current_version: String,
    pub latest_version: String,
}

/// Check if an update is available without prompting or downloading
#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
pub fn check_for_update() -> Result<Option<UpdateInfo>, Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> {
    use self_update::backends::github::ReleaseList;

    let releases = ReleaseList::configure()
        .repo_owner(REPO_OWNER)
        .repo_name(REPO_NAME)
        .build()?
        .fetch()?;

    let identifier = identifier();
    let current_version = cargo_crate_version!();

    let latest_release = releases.iter().find(|r| r.asset_for(TARGET, identifier).is_some());

    let latest_release = match latest_release {
        Some(r) => r,
        None => return Ok(None),
    };

    let latest_version = &latest_release.version;
    if !self_update::version::bump_is_greater(current_version, latest_version)? {
        return Ok(None);
    }

    Ok(Some(UpdateInfo {
        current_version: current_version.to_string(),
        latest_version: latest_version.clone(),
    }))
}

/// Spawn the new version after update and exit
pub fn spawn_updated_version() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> {
    let current_exe = env::current_exe()?;
    let mut command = Command::new(current_exe);
    command.args(env::args().skip(1)).env("NO_SELF_UPDATE", "1");

    command.spawn()?;

    std::process::exit(0);
}
```

`src/websocket.rs`:

```rs
use std::error::Error;
use std::io;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU16, AtomicUsize, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex as StdMutex};

use axum::extract::ws::{Message, Utf8Bytes, WebSocket, WebSocketUpgrade};
use axum::routing::get;
use axum::{Extension, Router};
use futures::lock::Mutex;
use futures::sink::SinkExt;
use futures::stream::{SplitSink, Stream, StreamExt};
use reliquary_archiver::export::Exporter;
use reliquary_archiver::export::fribbels::OptimizerEvent;
use serde::Serialize;
use tokio::sync::watch;
use tokio::task::AbortHandle;
use tokio_stream::wrappers::WatchStream;
use tracing::{debug, info, warn};

use crate::accounts::{AccountEvent, MultiAccountManager};

struct WebSocketServerState {
    pub manager: Arc<Mutex<MultiAccountManager>>,
    pub selected_account_rx: watch::Receiver<Option<u32>>,
    pub client_count: Arc<AtomicUsize>,
    pub client_count_tx: watch::Sender<usize>,
    pub service_handle: StdMutex<Option<AbortHandle>>,
    pub active_port: AtomicU16,
}

impl WebSocketServerState {
    /// Replaces the running server (if any) with a new one
    pub fn replace_service(&self, handle: AbortHandle, port: u16) {
        self.abort_service();
        self.active_port.store(port, Ordering::Relaxed);
        *self.service_handle.lock().unwrap() = Some(handle);
    }

    pub fn abort_service(&self) {
        if let Some(handle) = self.service_handle.lock().unwrap().take() {
            let current_port = self.active_port.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
            info!("Terminating WebSocket server on 0.0.0.0:{}", current_port);
            handle.abort();
        }
    }

    /// Sends the complete state of the account to the client, if there is one to send yet
    async fn send_initial_scan(&self, uid: u32, sender: &mut SplitSink<WebSocket, Message>) -> Result<(), Box<dyn Error>> {
        let Some(exporter) = self.manager.lock().await.get_account_exporter(uid) else {
            return Ok(());
        };

        let export = {
            let exporter = exporter.lock().await;
            if !exporter.is_initialized() {
                return Ok(());
            }
            exporter.export()
        };

        match export {
            Some(export) => send_serialized_message(sender, OptimizerEvent::InitialScan(export)).await,
            None => Ok(()),
        }
    }
}

pub enum PortSource {
    Fixed(u16),
    #[cfg(feature = "gui")]
    Dynamic(WatchStream<PortCommand>),
}

#[cfg(feature = "gui")]
#[derive(Clone)]
pub enum PortCommand {
    Open(u16),
    Close,
}

pub async fn start_websocket_server(
    #[cfg_attr(not(feature = "gui"), allow(unused_mut))] mut port_source: PortSource,
    manager: Arc<Mutex<MultiAccountManager>>,
    selected_account_rx: watch::Receiver<Option<u32>>,
) -> Result<(impl Stream<Item = Result<u16, String>>, impl Stream<Item = usize>), String> {
    let initial_port = match port_source {
        PortSource::Fixed(ref port) => *port,
        #[cfg(feature = "gui")]
        PortSource::Dynamic(_) => 0,
    };

    let (client_count_tx, client_count_rx) = watch::channel(0);
    let (port_tx, port_rx) = watch::channel::<Result<u16, String>>(Ok(initial_port));

    let state = Arc::new(WebSocketServerState {
        manager: manager.clone(),
        selected_account_rx,
        client_count: Arc::new(AtomicUsize::new(0)),
        client_count_tx,
        service_handle: StdMutex::new(None),
        active_port: AtomicU16::new(initial_port),
    });

    // Create streams from the watch receivers
    let client_count_stream = WatchStream::new(client_count_rx);
    // The port channel starts out with a placeholder, only report the ports the server actually runs on
    let port_stream = WatchStream::from_changes(port_rx);

    tokio::spawn(async move {
        loop {
            // Without the gui feature there is only a single kind of port source
            #[cfg_attr(not(feature = "gui"), allow(clippy::infallible_destructuring_match))]
            let port = match port_source {
                PortSource::Fixed(port) => port,
                #[cfg(feature = "gui")]
                PortSource::Dynamic(ref mut stream) => match stream.next().await {
                    None => break,
                    Some(PortCommand::Close) => {
                        state.abort_service();
                        continue;
                    }
                    Some(PortCommand::Open(port)) => port,
                },
            };

            let service = Router::new()
                .route("/ws", get(ws_handler))
                .layer(Extension(state.clone()))
                .into_make_service();

            let addr = std::net::SocketAddr::from(([0, 0, 0, 0], port));

            let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(addr).await.map_err(|e| match e.kind() {
                io::ErrorKind::AddrInUse => {
                    "Address already in use, please close any other instances of the application or try another address".to_string()
                }
                _ => e.to_string(),
            });

            match listener {
                Ok(listener) => {
                    let port = listener.local_addr().unwrap().port();
                    state.replace_service(
                        tokio::spawn(async move {
                            let local_addr = listener.local_addr().unwrap();
                            info!("Starting WebSocket server on {}", local_addr);
                            debug!("Listening on {}", local_addr);
                            axum::serve(listener, service).await.unwrap();
                        })
                        .abort_handle(),
                        port,
                    );
                    port_tx.send(Ok(port)).ok();
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    port_tx.send(Err(e)).ok();
                }
            }

            if matches!(port_source, PortSource::Fixed(_)) {
                break;
            };
        }
    });

    Ok((port_stream, client_count_stream))
}

async fn ws_handler(ws: WebSocketUpgrade, Extension(state): Extension<Arc<WebSocketServerState>>) -> axum::response::Response {
    ws.on_upgrade(|socket| handle_socket(socket, state))
}

async fn send_serialized_message<T: Serialize>(sender: &mut SplitSink<WebSocket, Message>, message: T) -> Result<(), Box<dyn Error>> {
    let message = serde_json::to_string(&message)?;
    sender.send(Message::Text(Utf8Bytes::from(message))).await?;

    Ok(())
}

async fn handle_socket(socket: WebSocket, state: Arc<WebSocketServerState>) {
    // Increment client count
    let client_count = state.client_count.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst) + 1;
    info!("New client connected, total clients: {}", client_count);

    // Notify GUI of client count change
    let _ = state.client_count_tx.send(client_count);

    let (mut sender, mut receiver) = socket.split();

    // Subscribe to manager's unified event channel
    let mut manager_rx = state.manager.lock().await.subscribe();
    let mut account_rx = state.selected_account_rx.clone();

    // Sync with GUI's current selection
    let mut selected_uid = *account_rx.borrow();

    // Send initial state if an account is already selected
    if let Some(uid) = selected_uid
        && let Err(e) = state.send_initial_scan(uid, &mut sender).await
    {
        warn!("Failed to send initial state: {}", e);
    }

    // Main loop: filter events by selected account and track GUI selection changes
    loop {
        tokio::select! {
            // Events from manager
            Ok(event) = manager_rx.recv() => {
                match event {
                    AccountEvent::ExporterEvent { uid, event } => {
                        // Only send if this is the selected account
                        if Some(uid) == selected_uid
                            && let Err(e) = send_serialized_message(&mut sender, event).await
                        {
                            warn!("Failed to send event to client: {}", e);
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    AccountEvent::Discovered { .. } | AccountEvent::Reconnected { .. } => {
                        // No-op, if the account is selected, the exporter will send the initial state on selection change.
                        // Or for the automatic initial export after exporter initialization.
                    }
                }
            }
            // GUI selection changed
            result = account_rx.changed() => {
                if result.is_err() {
                    break;
                }
                let new_uid = *account_rx.borrow_and_update();
                if new_uid != selected_uid {
                    selected_uid = new_uid;
                    info!("WebSocket switched to account {:?}", selected_uid);

                    // Send initial state for newly selected account
                    if let Some(uid) = selected_uid
                        && let Err(e) = state.send_initial_scan(uid, &mut sender).await
                    {
                        warn!("Failed to send initial state after selection: {}", e);
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
            // Client messages (for future account selection or keepalive)
            msg = receiver.next() => {
                if msg.is_none() {
                    // Client disconnected
                    info!("WebSocket client disconnected");
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Decrement client count when client disconnects
    let client_count = state.client_count.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::SeqCst) - 1;
    info!("Client disconnected, total clients: {}", client_count);

    // Notify GUI of client count change
    let _ = state.client_count_tx.send(client_count);
}

```
